## Uddaka son of Rāma

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
1241\. So, Aggivessana, seeking what is wholesome, searching for the unsurpassed state of supreme peace, I approached where Uddaka son of Rāma was. Having approached, I said this to Uddaka son of Rāma:

> ‘I wish, Āvuso, to lead the pure life in this Dhamma and Discipline.’

When this was said, Aggivessana, Uddaka son of Rāma said this to me:

> ‘May the venerable one stay; such is this Dhamma that a wise person, in no long time, having realised his own teacher's doctrine for himself through experiential understanding, may enter and dwell in it.’

So, Aggivessana, quickly, in no long time, I learned that Dhamma. So, Aggivessana, merely by lip-service, by mere verbal declaration, I would speak of the doctrine of knowledge and the doctrine of elders, and I would claim, ‘I know, I see,’ and so would others. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘It was not by mere faith alone that Rāma declared this Dhamma as something he entered and dwelt in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding; surely Rāma dwelt in this Dhamma knowing and seeing it.’

Then, Aggivessana, I approached where Uddaka son of Rāma was; having approached, I said this to Uddaka son of Rāma:

> ‘To what extent, Āvuso, did Rāma declare this Dhamma as something he entered and dwelt in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding?’

When this was said, Aggivessana, Uddaka son of Rāma declared the `nevasaññānāsaññāyatana` (base of neither apperception nor non-apperception). Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘It was not only Rāma who had faith; I also have faith; it was not only Rāma who had energy; I also have energy; it was not only Rāma who had awareness; I also have awareness; it was not only Rāma who had focus; I also have focus; it was not only Rāma who had wisdom; I also have wisdom. What if I were to strive for the realisation of that Dhamma which Rāma declared he entered and dwelt in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding?’

So, Aggivessana, quickly, in no long time, having realised that Dhamma for myself through experiential understanding, I entered and dwelt in it.

1242\. Then, Aggivessana, I approached where Uddaka son of Rāma was; having approached, I said this to Uddaka son of Rāma:

> ‘Was it to this extent, Āvuso, that Rāma declared this Dhamma, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it?’

> ‘To this extent indeed, Āvuso, did Rāma declare this Dhamma, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it.’

> ‘Indeed I too, Āvuso, to this extent enter and dwell in this Dhamma, having realised it for myself through experiential understanding.’

> ‘It is a gain for us, Āvuso, it is a great gain for us, that we see such a venerable one as a companion in the pure life. Thus, the Dhamma that Rāma declared, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it, that Dhamma you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding. The Dhamma that you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding, that Dhamma Rāma declared, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it. Thus, the Dhamma that Rāma experientially understood, that Dhamma you know; the Dhamma that you know, that Dhamma Rāma experientially understood. Thus, as Rāma was, so are you; as you are, so was Rāma. Come now, Āvuso, you lead this company.’

Thus, Aggivessana, Uddaka son of Rāma, being my companion in the pure life, placed me in the position of teacher, and honoured me with great honour. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘This Dhamma does not lead to giving up, nor to dispassion, nor to cessation, nor to calm, nor to experiential understanding, nor to full awakening, nor to extinguishment; it leads only to reappearance in the base of neither apperception nor non-apperception.’

So, Aggivessana, not valuing that Dhamma, having given up that Dhamma, I departed.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
1241\. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, kiṃkusalagavesī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyesamāno yena udako rāmaputto tenupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘icchāmahaṃ, āvuso, imasmiṃ dhammavinaye brahmacariyaṃ caritun’ti. Evaṃ vutte, aggivessana, udako rāmaputto maṃ etadavoca—  ‘viharatāyasmā, tādiso ayaṃ dhammo yattha viññū puriso nacirasseva sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyā’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, nacirasseva khippameva taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃ. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, tāvatakeneva oṭṭhapahatamattena lapitalāpanamattena ñāṇavādañca vadāmi theravādañca, ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti ca paṭijānāmi, ahañceva aññe ca. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘na kho rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ kevalaṃ saddhāmattakena sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesi. Addhā rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ vihāsī’ti. Atha khvāhaṃ, aggivessana, yena udako rāmaputto tenupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘kittāvatā no āvuso rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesī’ti? Evaṃ vutte, aggivessana, udako rāmaputto nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ pavedesi. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘na kho rāmasseva ahosi saddhā, mayhampatthi saddhā; na kho rāmasseva ahosi vīriyaṃ, mayhampatthi vīriyaṃ; na kho rāmasseva ahosi sati, mayhampatthi sati; na kho rāmasseva ahosi samādhi, mayhampatthi samādhi; na kho rāmasseva ahosi paññā, mayhampatthi paññā; yannūnāhaṃ yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesi tassa dhammassa sacchikiriyāya padaheyyan’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, nacirasseva khippameva taṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsiṃ.

1242\. Atha khvāhaṃ, aggivessana, yena udako rāmaputto tenupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘ettāvatā no āvuso rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesī’ti? ‘Ettāvatā kho āvuso rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesī’ti. ‘Ahampi kho, āvuso, ettāvatā imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmī’ti. ‘Lābhā no, āvuso, suladdhaṃ no, āvuso, ye mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ tādisaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ passāma. Iti yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesi, taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharasi; yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharasi, taṃ dhammaṃ rāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesi. Iti yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmo abhiññāsi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi; yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi taṃ dhammaṃ rāmo abhiññāsi. Iti yādiso rāmo ahosi tādiso tuvaṃ; yādiso tuvaṃ tādiso rāmo ahosi. Ehi dāni, āvuso, tuvaṃ imaṃ gaṇaṃ pariharā’ti. Iti kho, aggivessana, udako rāmaputto sabrahmacārī me samāno ācariyaṭṭhāne ca maṃ ṭhapesi, uḷārāya ca maṃ pūjāya pūjesi. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘nāyaṃ dhammo nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, yāvadeva nevasaññānāsaññāyatanūpapattiyā’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, taṃ dhammaṃ analaṅkaritvā tasmā dhammā nibbijja apakkamiṃ.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
1241\. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀓𑀺𑀁𑀓𑀼𑀲𑀮𑀕𑀯𑁂𑀲𑀻 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀯𑀭𑀧𑀤𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁; 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—  ‘𑀇𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸𑀫𑀳𑀁, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀇𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀯𑀺𑀦𑀬𑁂 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀢𑀼𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀫𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘—  ‘𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸, 𑀢𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀬𑀢𑁆𑀣 𑀯𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀽 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀦𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀲𑀓𑀁 𑀆𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀓𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀦𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀧𑀼𑀡𑀺𑀁. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀓𑁂𑀦𑁂𑀯 𑀑𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀧𑀳𑀢𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀮𑀧𑀺𑀢𑀮𑀸𑀧𑀦𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀯𑀸𑀤𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀯𑀤𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀣𑁂𑀭𑀯𑀸𑀤𑀜𑁆𑀘, ‘𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀻’𑀢𑀺 𑀘 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀅𑀳𑀜𑁆𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑁂 𑀘. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀓𑁂𑀯𑀮𑀁 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀓𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀺. 𑀅𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀲𑀻’𑀢𑀺. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁; 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—  ‘𑀓𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀦𑁄 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀻’𑀢𑀺? 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀦𑁂𑀯𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀬𑀢𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸; 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀯𑀻𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀯𑀻𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁; 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀲𑀢𑀺; 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀥𑀺, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀥𑀺; 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸; 𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀺 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀺𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀤𑀳𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀦𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁.

1242\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁; 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—  ‘𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀦𑁄 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀻’𑀢𑀺? ‘𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀻’𑀢𑀺. ‘𑀅𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻’𑀢𑀺. ‘𑀮𑀸𑀪𑀸 𑀦𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀲𑀼𑀮𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀁 𑀦𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀬𑁂 𑀫𑀬𑀁 𑀆𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀢𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑀁 𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫. 𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀺, 𑀢𑀁 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀲𑀺; 𑀬𑀁 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀲𑀺, 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀺. 𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺 𑀢𑀁 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀺; 𑀬𑀁 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀺 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺. 𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀢𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀢𑀼𑀯𑀁; 𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀢𑀼𑀯𑀁 𑀢𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺. 𑀏𑀳𑀺 𑀤𑀸𑀦𑀺, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀢𑀼𑀯𑀁 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀕𑀡𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀔𑁄, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀻 𑀫𑁂 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀆𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸𑀦𑁂 𑀘 𑀫𑀁 𑀞𑀧𑁂𑀲𑀺, 𑀉𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀸𑀬 𑀘 𑀫𑀁 𑀧𑀽𑀚𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀽𑀚𑁂𑀲𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀺𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀯𑀺𑀭𑀸𑀕𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀦𑀺𑀭𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀁𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀢𑀺, 𑀬𑀸𑀯𑀤𑁂𑀯 𑀦𑁂𑀯𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀬𑀢𑀦𑀽𑀧𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀮𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀭𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀅𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁.
</TabItem>
<TabItem label="Interlinear">
1241.1 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, kiṃkusalagavesī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyesamāno yena udako rāmaputto tenupasaṅkamiṃ;  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀①🆎(kiṃ+kusala+gavesī) 🚻⨀②🆎(na+uttara) 🚻⨀②🆎(santi+vara+pada) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(pariyesamāna) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(udaka) 🚹⨀①(rāmaputta) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(upasaṅkamati)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | seeking what is wholesome | unsurpassed | state of supreme peace | searching for | by where | Udaka | Rāmaputta | by there | I approached  
So, Aggivessana, seeking what is wholesome, searching for the unsurpassed state of supreme peace, I approached where Uddaka son of Rāma was.

1241.2 upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘icchāmahaṃ, āvuso, imasmiṃ dhammavinaye brahmacariyaṃ caritun’ti.  
🔽(tvā)(upasaṅkamati) 🚹⨀②(udaka) 🚹⨀②(rāmaputta) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(vadati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(icchati) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑦(ima) 🚹⨀⑦🆎(dhamma+vinaya) 🚻⨀②🆎(brahma+cariya) 🔽(tuṃ)(carati) ⏏️(iti)  
having approached | Udaka | Rāmaputta | this | I said | I wish | I | Āvuso | in this | Dhamma and Discipline | the pure life | to lead | (end quote)  
Having approached, I said this to Uddaka son of Rāma: ‘I wish, Āvuso, to lead the pure life in this Dhamma and Discipline.’

1241.3 Evaṃ vutte, aggivessana, udako rāmaputto maṃ etadavoca—  ‘viharatāyasmā, tādiso ayaṃ dhammo yattha viññū puriso nacirasseva sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyā’ti.  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(vutta) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀①(udaka) 🚹⨀①(rāmaputta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(vadati) 🟢⨀🤟⏹(viharati) 🚹⨀①(āyasmant) 🚹⨀①(tādisa) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①(dhamma) ⏏️(yattha) 🚹⨀①(viññū) 🚹⨀①(purisa) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(cirassa) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀②(saka) 🚹⨀②(ācariyaka) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(viharati) ⏏️(iti)  
thus | said | Aggivessana | Udaka | Rāmaputta | me | this | said | may stay | the venerable one | such | this | Dhamma | in which | wise | person | not | after a long time | just | his own | teacher's doctrine | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | may dwell | (end quote)  
When this was said, Aggivessana, Uddaka son of Rāma said this to me: ‘May the venerable one stay; such is this Dhamma that a wise person, in no long time, having realised his own teacher's doctrine for himself through experiential understanding, may enter and dwell in it.’

1241.4 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, nacirasseva khippameva taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(cirassa) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀②(khippa) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟢⨀👆⏮(pariyāpuṇāti)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | not | after a long time | just | quickly | just | that | Dhamma | I learned  
So, Aggivessana, quickly, in no long time, I learned that Dhamma.

1241.5 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, tāvatakeneva oṭṭhapahatamattena lapitalāpanamattena ñāṇavādañca vadāmi theravādañca, ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti ca paṭijānāmi, ahañceva aññe ca.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚻⨀③(tāvataka) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀③🆎(oṭṭha+pahata+matta) 🚻⨀③🆎(lapita+lāpana+matta) 🚹⨀②🆎(ñāṇa+vāda) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀👆▶️(vadati) 🚹⨀②🆎(thera+vāda) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀👆▶️(jānāti) 🟢⨀👆▶️(passati) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀👆▶️(paṭijānāti) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(añña) ⏏️(ca)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | by just so much | merely | by mere lip-service | by mere verbal declaration | the doctrine of knowledge | and | I speak of | the doctrine of elders | and | I know | I see | (end quote) | and | I claim | I | and | indeed | others | and  
So, Aggivessana, merely by lip-service, by mere verbal declaration, I would speak of the doctrine of knowledge and the doctrine of elders, and I would claim, ‘I know, I see,’ and so would others.

1241.6 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘na kho rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ kevalaṃ saddhāmattakena sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesi.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(rāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚻⨀②(kevala) 🚻⨀③🆎(saddhā+mattaka) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | was | not | indeed | Rāma | this | Dhamma | only | by mere faith | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I dwell in | (end quote) | declared  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘It was not by mere faith alone that Rāma declared this Dhamma as something he entered and dwelt in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding;

1241.7 Addhā rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ vihāsī’ti.  
⏏️(addhā) 🚹⨀①(rāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(anta)(jānanta) 🚹⨀①🔽(anta)(passanta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(viharati) ⏏️(iti)  
surely | Rāma | this | Dhamma | knowing | seeing | dwelt in | (end quote)  
surely Rāma dwelt in this Dhamma knowing and seeing it.’

1241.8 Atha khvāhaṃ, aggivessana, yena udako rāmaputto tenupasaṅkamiṃ;  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(udaka) 🚹⨀①(rāmaputta) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(upasaṅkamati)  
then | indeed | I | Aggivessana | by where | Udaka | Rāmaputta | by there | I approached  
Then, Aggivessana, I approached where Uddaka son of Rāma was;

1241.9 upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘kittāvatā no āvuso rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesī’ti?  
🔽(tvā)(upasaṅkamati) 🚹⨀②(udaka) 🚹⨀②(rāmaputta) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(vadati) ⏏️(kittāvatā) ⏏️(no) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚹⨀①(rāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti) ⏏️(iti)  
having approached | Udaka | Rāmaputta | this | I said | to what extent | then | Āvuso | Rāma | this | Dhamma | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I dwell in | (end quote) | declared | (end quote)  
having approached, I said this to Uddaka son of Rāma: ‘To what extent, Āvuso, did Rāma declare this Dhamma as something he entered and dwelt in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding?’

1241.10 Evaṃ vutte, aggivessana, udako rāmaputto nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ pavedesi.  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(vutta) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀①(udaka) 🚹⨀①(rāmaputta) 🚻⨀②🆎(na+eva+saññā+na+saññā+āyatana) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti)  
thus | when said | Aggivessana | Udaka | Rāmaputta | the base of neither apperception nor non-apperception | declared  
When this was said, Aggivessana, Uddaka son of Rāma declared the `nevasaññānāsaññāyatana` (base of neither apperception nor non-apperception).

1241.11 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘na kho rāmasseva ahosi saddhā, mayhampatthi saddhā;  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⑥(rāma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🚺⨀①(saddhā) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚺⨀①(saddhā)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | was | not | indeed | of Rāma | only | was | faith | of me | also | there is | faith  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘It was not only Rāma who had faith; I also have faith;

1241.12 na kho rāmasseva ahosi vīriyaṃ, mayhampatthi vīriyaṃ;

it was not only Rāma who had energy; I also have energy;

⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⑥(rāma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🚺⨀①(sati) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚺⨀①(sati)  
not | indeed | of Rāma | only | was | awareness | of me | also | there is | awareness  
it was not only Rāma who had awareness; I also have awareness;

not | indeed | of Rāma | only | was | focus | of me | also | there is | focus  
it was not only Rāma who had focus; I also have focus;

1241.15 na kho rāmasseva ahosi paññā, mayhampatthi paññā;

1241.16 yannūnāhaṃ yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesi tassa dhammassa sacchikiriyāya padaheyyan’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ya) ⏏️(nūna) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀①(rāma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(ta) 🚹⨀⑥(dhamma) 🚺⨀④🆎(sacchi+kiriyā) 🔵⨀👆⏯(padahati) ⏏️(iti)

What if I were to strive for the realisation of that Dhamma which Rāma declared he entered and dwelt in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding?’

1241.17 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, nacirasseva khippameva taṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(cirassa) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀②(khippa) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆⏮(viharati)

So, Aggivessana, quickly, in no long time, having realised that Dhamma for myself through experiential understanding, I entered and dwelt in it.

1242.1 Atha khvāhaṃ, aggivessana, yena udako rāmaputto tenupasaṅkamiṃ;  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(udaka) 🚹⨀①(rāmaputta) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(upasaṅkamati)

Then, Aggivessana, I approached where Uddaka son of Rāma was;

1242.2 upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘ettāvatā no āvuso rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesī’ti?

having approached, I said this to Uddaka son of Rāma: ‘Was it to this extent, Āvuso, that Rāma declared this Dhamma, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it?’

1242.3 ‘Ettāvatā kho āvuso rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesī’ti.  
⏏️(ettāvatā) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚹⨀①(rāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti) ⏏️(iti)

‘To this extent indeed, Āvuso, did Rāma declare this Dhamma, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it.’

1242.4 ‘Ahampi kho, āvuso, ettāvatā imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmī’ti.  
*️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(āvuso) ⏏️(ettāvatā) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti)

‘Indeed I too, Āvuso, to this extent enter and dwell in this Dhamma, having realised it for myself through experiential understanding.’

1242.5 ‘Lābhā no, āvuso, suladdhaṃ no, āvuso, ye mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ tādisaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ passāma.  
⏏️(lābhā) *️⃣👆⚧⨂④(ahaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) ⏏️(suladdhaṃ) *️⃣👆⚧⨂④(ahaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ya) *️⃣👆⚧⨂①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀②(āyasmant) 🚹⨀②(tādisa) 🚹⨀②🆎(sa+brahmacārī) 🟢⨂👆▶️(passati)

‘It is a gain for us, Āvuso, it is a great gain for us, that we see such a venerable one as a companion in the pure life.

1242.6 Iti yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesi, taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharasi;

Thus, the Dhamma that Rāma declared, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it, that Dhamma you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding.

1242.7 yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharasi, taṃ dhammaṃ rāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesi.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(viharati) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀①(rāma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti)  
which | you | Dhamma | for yourself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | you dwell in | that | Dhamma | Rāma | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | declared  
The Dhamma that you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding, that Dhamma Rāma declared, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it.

1242.8 Iti yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmo abhiññāsi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi;

Thus, the Dhamma that Rāma experientially understood, that Dhamma you know;

1242.9 yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi taṃ dhammaṃ rāmo abhiññāsi.

which | you | Dhamma | you know | that | Dhamma | Rāma | experientially understood  
the Dhamma that you know, that Dhamma Rāma experientially understood.

1242.10 Iti yādiso rāmo ahosi tādiso tuvaṃ;  
⏏️(iti) 🚹⨀①(yādisa) 🚹⨀①(rāma) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🚹⨀①(tādisa) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ)  
thus | of whatever kind | Rāma | was | of such kind | you  
Thus, as Rāma was, so are you;

1242.11 yādiso tuvaṃ tādiso rāmo ahosi.  
🚹⨀①(yādisa) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀①(tādisa) 🚹⨀①(rāma) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti)  
of whatever kind | you | of such kind | Rāma | was  
as you are, so was Rāma.

1242.12 Ehi dāni, āvuso, tuvaṃ imaṃ gaṇaṃ pariharā’ti.  
🟢⨀🤘⏹(eti) ⏏️(dāni) ⏏️(āvuso) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(gaṇa) 🟢⨀🤘⏹(pariharati) ⏏️(iti)  
come | now | Āvuso | you | this | company | lead | (end quote)  
Come now, Āvuso, you lead this company.’

1242.13 Iti kho, aggivessana, udako rāmaputto sabrahmacārī me samāno ācariyaṭṭhāne ca maṃ ṭhapesi, uḷārāya ca maṃ pūjāya pūjesi.  
⏏️(iti) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀①(udaka) 🚹⨀①(rāmaputta) 🚹⨀①🆎(sa+brahmacārī) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(ācariya+ṭhāna) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(ṭhapeti) 🚺⨀③(uḷāra) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚺⨀③(pūjā) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(pūjeti)  
thus | indeed | Aggivessana | Udaka | Rāmaputta | companion in the pure life | my | being | in the position of teacher | and | me | placed | with great | and | me | honour | honoured  
Thus, Aggivessana, Uddaka son of Rāma, being my companion in the pure life, placed me in the position of teacher, and honoured me with great honour.

1242.14 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘nāyaṃ dhammo nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, yāvadeva nevasaññānāsaññāyatanūpapattiyā’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) ⏏️(na) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①(dhamma) 🚺⨀④(nibbidā) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀④(virāga) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀④(nirodha) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀④(upasama) ⏏️(na) 🚺⨀④(abhiññā) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀④(sambodha) ⏏️(na) 🚻⨀④(nibbāna) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(saṃvattati) ⏏️(yāva) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀④🆎(na+eva+saññā+na+saññā+āyatana+upapatti) ⏏️(iti)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | was | not | this | Dhamma | to giving up | nor | to detachment | nor | to cessation | nor | to calm | nor | to experiential understanding | nor | to full awakening | nor | to extinguishment | leads | as far as | only | to reappearance in the base of neither apperception nor non-apperception | (end quote)  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘This Dhamma does not lead to giving up, nor to dispassion, nor to cessation, nor to calm, nor to experiential understanding, nor to full awakening, nor to extinguishment; it leads only to reappearance in the base of neither apperception nor non-apperception.’

1242.15 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, taṃ dhammaṃ analaṅkaritvā tasmā dhammā nibbijja apakkamiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🔽(tvā)🆎(na+alaṅkaroti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑤(ta) 🚹⨀⑤(dhamma) 🔽(ya)(nibbindati) 🟢⨀👆⏮(apakkamati)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | that | Dhamma | not valuing | from that | Dhamma | having given up | I departed  
So, Aggivessana, not valuing that Dhamma, having given up that Dhamma, I departed.

## The Three Similes

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
1243\. So, Aggivessana, I, seeking what is wholesome, searching for the unsurpassed state of supreme peace, wandering by stages through Magadha, arrived at Uruvelā, the army township. There I saw a delightful stretch of land, a beautiful grove, a clear-flowing river with pleasant banks, and a village for alms around it. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘Indeed, delightful is this stretch of land, beautiful is this grove, clear-flowing is this river with pleasant banks, and a village for alms is around it. This is indeed suitable for a young man of good family intent on striving.’

So, Aggivessana, I sat down right there, thinking,

> ‘This is enough for striving.’

1244\. Then, Aggivessana, three similes occurred to me, unheard-before and wonderful.

### First Simile

Just as, Aggivessana, if there were a wet sappy log placed in water, and a man came with an upper fire-stick, thinking:

> ‘I will light a fire, I will make heat appear.’ Aggivessana, what do you consider: could that man, by rubbing the wet sappy log placed in water with the upper fire-stick, light a fire and make heat appear?”

> “Of course not, Bho Gotama.”

> “For what reason?”

> “Because, Bho Gotama, that log is wet and sappy, and moreover it is placed in water. That man would only reap weariness and vexation.”

> “Exactly in the same way, Aggivessana, whatever `samaṇa` (wanderers) or brahmins dwell not withdrawn from sensual pleasures in body and mind, and their internal desire for sensual pleasures, affection for sensual pleasures, infatuation with sensual pleasures, thirst for sensual pleasures, fever for sensual pleasures is not fully given up and fully stilled — even if those worthy religious practitioners feel painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, they are incapable of knowledge and vision and unsurpassed full awakening. And even if those worthy religious practitioners do not feel painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, they are incapable of knowledge and vision and unsurpassed full awakening. This, Aggivessana, was the first simile that occurred to me, unheard-before and wonderful.

### Second Simile

1245\. Then, Aggivessana, a second simile occurred to me, unheard-before and wonderful. Just as, Aggivessana, if there were a wet sappy log placed on dry land far from water. And a man came with an upper fire-stick, thinking:

> ‘I will light a fire, I will make heat appear.’ Aggivessana, what do you consider: could that man, by rubbing the wet sappy log placed on dry land far from water with the upper fire-stick, light a fire and make heat appear?”

> “Of course not, Bho Gotama.”

> “For what reason?”

> “Because, Bho Gotama, that log is wet and sappy, even though it is placed on dry land far from water. That man would only reap weariness and vexation.”

> “Exactly in the same way, Aggivessana, whatever wanderers or brahmins dwell withdrawn from sensual pleasures in body and mind, but their internal desire for sensual pleasures, affection for sensual pleasures, infatuation with sensual pleasures, thirst for sensual pleasures, fever for sensual pleasures is not fully given up and fully stilled — even if those worthy religious practitioners feel painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, they are incapable of knowledge and vision and unsurpassed full awakening. And even if those worthy religious practitioners do not feel painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, they are incapable of knowledge and vision and unsurpassed full awakening. This, Aggivessana, was the second simile that occurred to me, unheard-before and wonderful.

### Third Simile

1246\. Then, Aggivessana, a third simile occurred to me, unheard-before and wonderful. Just as, Aggivessana, if there were a dry, sapless log placed on dry land far from water. And a man came with an upper fire-stick, thinking:

> ‘I will light a fire, I will make heat appear.’ Aggivessana, what do you consider: could that man, by rubbing the dry, sapless log placed on dry land far from water with the upper fire-stick, light a fire and make heat appear?”

> “Yes, Bho Gotama.”

> “For what reason?”

> “Because, Bho Gotama, that log is dry and sapless, and moreover it is placed on dry land far from water.”

“Exactly in the same way, Aggivessana, whatever wanderers or brahmins dwell withdrawn from sensual pleasures in body and mind, and their internal desire for sensual pleasures, affection for sensual pleasures, infatuation with sensual pleasures, thirst for sensual pleasures, fever for sensual pleasures is fully given up and fully stilled — if those worthy religious practitioners feel painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, they are capable of knowledge and vision and unsurpassed full awakening. And if those worthy religious practitioners do not feel painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, they are capable of knowledge and vision and unsurpassed full awakening. This, Aggivessana, was the third simile that occurred to me, unheard-before and wonderful. These three similes, Aggivessana, occurred to me, unheard-before and wonderful.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
1243\. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, kiṃkusalagavesī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyesamāno magadhesu anupubbena cārikaṃ caramāno yena uruvelā senānigamo tadavasariṃ. Tatthaddasaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ bhūmibhāgaṃ, pāsādikañca vanasaṇḍaṃ, nadiñca sandantiṃ setakaṃ supatitthaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ, samantā ca gocaragāmaṃ. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘ramaṇīyo vata bho bhūmibhāgo, pāsādiko ca vanasaṇḍo, nadī ca sandati setakā supatitthā ramaṇīyā, samantā ca gocaragāmo. Alaṃ vatidaṃ kulaputtassa padhānatthikassa padhānāyā’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, tattheva nisīdiṃ ‘alamidaṃ padhānāyā’ti.

1244\. Apissumaṃ, aggivessana, tisso upamā paṭibhaṃsu anacchariyā pubbe assutapubbā. Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, allaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnehaṃ udake nikkhittaṃ. Atha puriso āgaccheyya uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya—  ‘aggiṃ abhinibbattessāmi, tejo pātukarissāmī’ti. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi, aggivessana, api nu so puriso amuṃ allaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnehaṃ, udake nikkhittaṃ, uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya abhimanthento aggiṃ abhinibbatteyya, tejo pātukareyyā”ti? “No hidaṃ, bho gotama”. “Taṃ kissa hetu”? “Aduñhi, bho gotama, allaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnehaṃ, tañca pana udake nikkhittaṃ. Yāvadeva ca pana so puriso kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā”ti. “Evameva kho, aggivessana, ye hi keci samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā kāyena ceva cittena ca kāmehi avūpakaṭṭhā viharanti, yo ca nesaṃ kāmesu kāmacchando kāmasneho kāmamucchā kāmapipāsā kāmapariḷāho, so ca ajjhattaṃ na suppahīno hoti, na suppaṭippassaddho, opakkamikā cepi te bhonto samaṇabrāhmaṇā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayanti, abhabbāva te ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambodhāya. No cepi te bhonto samaṇabrāhmaṇā opakkamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayanti, abhabbāva te ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambodhāya. Ayaṃ kho maṃ, aggivessana, paṭhamā upamā paṭibhāsi anacchariyā pubbe assutapubbā.

1245\. Aparāpi kho maṃ, aggivessana, dutiyā upamā paṭibhāsi anacchariyā pubbe assutapubbā. Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, allaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnehaṃ, ārakā udakā thale nikkhittaṃ. Atha puriso āgaccheyya uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya—  ‘aggiṃ abhinibbattessāmi, tejo pātukarissāmī’ti. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi, aggivessana, api nu so puriso amuṃ allaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnehaṃ, ārakā udakā thale nikkhittaṃ, uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya abhimanthento aggiṃ abhinibbatteyya tejo pātukareyyā”ti? “No hidaṃ, bho gotama”. “Taṃ kissa hetu”? “Aduñhi, bho gotama, allaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnehaṃ, kiñcāpi ārakā udakā thale nikkhittaṃ. Yāvadeva ca pana so puriso kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā”ti. “Evameva kho, aggivessana, ye hi keci samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā kāyena ceva cittena ca kāmehi vūpakaṭṭhā viharanti, yo ca nesaṃ kāmesu kāmacchando kāmasneho kāmamucchā kāmapipāsā kāmapariḷāho so ca ajjhattaṃ na suppahīno hoti, na suppaṭippassaddho, opakkamikā cepi te bhonto samaṇabrāhmaṇā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayanti, abhabbāva te ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambodhāya. No cepi te bhonto samaṇabrāhmaṇā opakkamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayanti, abhabbāva te ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambodhāya. Ayaṃ kho maṃ, aggivessana, dutiyā upamā paṭibhāsi anacchariyā pubbe assutapubbā

1246\. Aparāpi kho maṃ, aggivessana, tatiyā upamā paṭibhāsi anacchariyā pubbe assutapubbā. Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, sukkhaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ koḷāpaṃ, ārakā udakā thale nikkhittaṃ. Atha puriso āgaccheyya uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya—  ‘aggiṃ abhinibbattessāmi, tejo pātukarissāmī’ti. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi, aggivessana, api nu so puriso amuṃ sukkhaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ koḷāpaṃ, ārakā udakā thale nikkhittaṃ, uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya abhimanthento aggiṃ abhinibbatteyya, tejo pātukareyyā”ti? “Evaṃ, bho gotama”. “Taṃ kissa hetu”? “Aduñhi, bho gotama, sukkhaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ koḷāpaṃ, tañca pana ārakā udakā thale nikkhittan”ti. “Evameva kho, aggivessana, ye hi keci samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā kāyena ceva cittena ca kāmehi vūpakaṭṭhā viharanti, yo ca nesaṃ kāmesu kāmacchando kāmasneho kāmamucchā kāmapipāsā kāmapariḷāho, so ca ajjhattaṃ suppahīno hoti suppaṭippassaddho, opakkamikā cepi te bhonto samaṇabrāhmaṇā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayanti, bhabbāva te ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambodhāya. No cepi te bhonto samaṇabrāhmaṇā opakkamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayanti, bhabbāva te ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambodhāya. Ayaṃ kho maṃ, aggivessana, tatiyā upamā paṭibhāsi anacchariyā pubbe assutapubbā. Imā kho maṃ, aggivessana, tisso upamā paṭibhaṃsu anacchariyā pubbe assutapubbā.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
1243\. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀓𑀺𑀁𑀓𑀼𑀲𑀮𑀕𑀯𑁂𑀲𑀻 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀯𑀭𑀧𑀤𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀫𑀕𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂𑀦 𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀁 𑀘𑀭𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀉𑀭𑀼𑀯𑁂𑀮𑀸 𑀲𑁂𑀦𑀸𑀦𑀺𑀕𑀫𑁄 𑀢𑀤𑀯𑀲𑀭𑀺𑀁. 𑀢𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀲𑀁 𑀭𑀫𑀡𑀻𑀬𑀁 𑀪𑀽𑀫𑀺𑀪𑀸𑀕𑀁, 𑀧𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀓𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀯𑀦𑀲𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀁, 𑀦𑀤𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀤𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀁 𑀲𑁂𑀢𑀓𑀁 𑀲𑀼𑀧𑀢𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀁 𑀭𑀫𑀡𑀻𑀬𑀁, 𑀲𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀘 𑀕𑁄𑀘𑀭𑀕𑀸𑀫𑀁. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀭𑀫𑀡𑀻𑀬𑁄 𑀯𑀢 𑀪𑁄 𑀪𑀽𑀫𑀺𑀪𑀸𑀕𑁄, 𑀧𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀓𑁄 𑀘 𑀯𑀦𑀲𑀡𑁆𑀟𑁄, 𑀦𑀤𑀻 𑀘 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀤𑀢𑀺 𑀲𑁂𑀢𑀓𑀸 𑀲𑀼𑀧𑀢𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸 𑀭𑀫𑀡𑀻𑀬𑀸, 𑀲𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀘 𑀕𑁄𑀘𑀭𑀕𑀸𑀫𑁄. 𑀅𑀮𑀁 𑀯𑀢𑀺𑀤𑀁 𑀓𑀼𑀮𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀢𑀢𑁆𑀣𑁂𑀯 𑀦𑀺𑀲𑀻𑀤𑀺𑀁 ‘𑀅𑀮𑀫𑀺𑀤𑀁 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺.

1244\. 𑀅𑀧𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀢𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁄 𑀉𑀧𑀫𑀸 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀪𑀁𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀦𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀢𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀅𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀁 𑀓𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀲𑀲𑁆𑀦𑁂𑀳𑀁 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁂 𑀦𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁. 𑀅𑀣 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀆𑀕𑀘𑁆𑀙𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀭𑀡𑀺𑀁 𑀆𑀤𑀸𑀬—  ‘𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀢𑁂𑀚𑁄 𑀧𑀸𑀢𑀼𑀓𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀻’𑀢𑀺. 𑀢𑀁 𑀓𑀺𑀁 𑀫𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀲𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀅𑀧𑀺 𑀦𑀼 𑀲𑁄 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀅𑀫𑀼𑀁 𑀅𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀁 𑀓𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀲𑀲𑁆𑀦𑁂𑀳𑀁, 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁂 𑀦𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁, 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀭𑀡𑀺𑀁 𑀆𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀣𑁂𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬, 𑀢𑁂𑀚𑁄 𑀧𑀸𑀢𑀼𑀓𑀭𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀸”𑀢𑀺? “𑀦𑁄 𑀳𑀺𑀤𑀁, 𑀪𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫”. “𑀢𑀁 𑀓𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀳𑁂𑀢𑀼”? “𑀅𑀤𑀼𑀜𑁆𑀳𑀺, 𑀪𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫, 𑀅𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀁 𑀓𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀲𑀲𑁆𑀦𑁂𑀳𑀁, 𑀢𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁂 𑀦𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁. 𑀬𑀸𑀯𑀤𑁂𑀯 𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀲𑁄 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀓𑀺𑀮𑀫𑀣𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀯𑀺𑀖𑀸𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀪𑀸𑀕𑀻 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸”𑀢𑀺. “𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀬𑁂 𑀳𑀺 𑀓𑁂𑀘𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀯𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀘 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀅𑀯𑀽𑀧𑀓𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀬𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑁂𑀲𑀁 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀦𑁂𑀳𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀺𑀧𑀸𑀲𑀸 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀍𑀆𑀳𑁄, 𑀲𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀲𑀼𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀳𑀻𑀦𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺, 𑀦 𑀲𑀼𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄, 𑀑𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀘𑁂𑀧𑀺 𑀢𑁂 𑀪𑁄𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀢𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸 𑀔𑀭𑀸 𑀓𑀝𑀼𑀓𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀪𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀯 𑀢𑁂 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀸𑀬 𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬. 𑀦𑁄 𑀘𑁂𑀧𑀺 𑀢𑁂 𑀪𑁄𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀑𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀢𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸 𑀔𑀭𑀸 𑀓𑀝𑀼𑀓𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀪𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀯 𑀢𑁂 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀸𑀬 𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬. 𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀧𑀞𑀫𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀫𑀸 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀪𑀸𑀲𑀺 𑀅𑀦𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀢𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸.

1245\. 𑀅𑀧𑀭𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀤𑀼𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀫𑀸 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀪𑀸𑀲𑀺 𑀅𑀦𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀢𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀅𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀁 𑀓𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀲𑀲𑁆𑀦𑁂𑀳𑀁, 𑀆𑀭𑀓𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀸 𑀣𑀮𑁂 𑀦𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁. 𑀅𑀣 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀆𑀕𑀘𑁆𑀙𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀭𑀡𑀺𑀁 𑀆𑀤𑀸𑀬—  ‘𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀢𑁂𑀚𑁄 𑀧𑀸𑀢𑀼𑀓𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀻’𑀢𑀺. 𑀢𑀁 𑀓𑀺𑀁 𑀫𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀲𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀅𑀧𑀺 𑀦𑀼 𑀲𑁄 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀅𑀫𑀼𑀁 𑀅𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀁 𑀓𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀲𑀲𑁆𑀦𑁂𑀳𑀁, 𑀆𑀭𑀓𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀸 𑀣𑀮𑁂 𑀦𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁, 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀭𑀡𑀺𑀁 𑀆𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀣𑁂𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬 𑀢𑁂𑀚𑁄 𑀧𑀸𑀢𑀼𑀓𑀭𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀸”𑀢𑀺? “𑀦𑁄 𑀳𑀺𑀤𑀁, 𑀪𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫”. “𑀢𑀁 𑀓𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀳𑁂𑀢𑀼”? “𑀅𑀤𑀼𑀜𑁆𑀳𑀺, 𑀪𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫, 𑀅𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀁 𑀓𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀲𑀲𑁆𑀦𑁂𑀳𑀁, 𑀓𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀆𑀭𑀓𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀸 𑀣𑀮𑁂 𑀦𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁. 𑀬𑀸𑀯𑀤𑁂𑀯 𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀲𑁄 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀓𑀺𑀮𑀫𑀣𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀯𑀺𑀖𑀸𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀪𑀸𑀕𑀻 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸”𑀢𑀺. “𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀬𑁂 𑀳𑀺 𑀓𑁂𑀘𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀯𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀘 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀯𑀽𑀧𑀓𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀬𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑁂𑀲𑀁 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀦𑁂𑀳𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀺𑀧𑀸𑀲𑀸 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀍𑀆𑀳𑁄 𑀲𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀲𑀼𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀳𑀻𑀦𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺, 𑀦 𑀲𑀼𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄, 𑀑𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀘𑁂𑀧𑀺 𑀢𑁂 𑀪𑁄𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀢𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸 𑀔𑀭𑀸 𑀓𑀝𑀼𑀓𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀪𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀯 𑀢𑁂 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀸𑀬 𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬. 𑀦𑁄 𑀘𑁂𑀧𑀺 𑀢𑁂 𑀪𑁄𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀑𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀢𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸 𑀔𑀭𑀸 𑀓𑀝𑀼𑀓𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀪𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀯 𑀢𑁂 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀸𑀬 𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬. 𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀤𑀼𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀫𑀸 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀪𑀸𑀲𑀺 𑀅𑀦𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀢𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸

1246\. 𑀅𑀧𑀭𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀢𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀫𑀸 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀪𑀸𑀲𑀺 𑀅𑀦𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀢𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀲𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀁 𑀓𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀓𑁄𑀍𑀆𑀧𑀁, 𑀆𑀭𑀓𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀸 𑀣𑀮𑁂 𑀦𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁. 𑀅𑀣 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀆𑀕𑀘𑁆𑀙𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀭𑀡𑀺𑀁 𑀆𑀤𑀸𑀬—  ‘𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀢𑁂𑀚𑁄 𑀧𑀸𑀢𑀼𑀓𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀻’𑀢𑀺. 𑀢𑀁 𑀓𑀺𑀁 𑀫𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀲𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀅𑀧𑀺 𑀦𑀼 𑀲𑁄 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀅𑀫𑀼𑀁 𑀲𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀁 𑀓𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀓𑁄𑀍𑀆𑀧𑀁, 𑀆𑀭𑀓𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀸 𑀣𑀮𑁂 𑀦𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁, 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀭𑀡𑀺𑀁 𑀆𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀣𑁂𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬, 𑀢𑁂𑀚𑁄 𑀧𑀸𑀢𑀼𑀓𑀭𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀸”𑀢𑀺? “𑀏𑀯𑀁, 𑀪𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫”. “𑀢𑀁 𑀓𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀳𑁂𑀢𑀼”? “𑀅𑀤𑀼𑀜𑁆𑀳𑀺, 𑀪𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫, 𑀲𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀁 𑀓𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀓𑁄𑀍𑀆𑀧𑀁, 𑀢𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀆𑀭𑀓𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀸 𑀣𑀮𑁂 𑀦𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑁆”𑀢𑀺. “𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀬𑁂 𑀳𑀺 𑀓𑁂𑀘𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀯𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀘 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀯𑀽𑀧𑀓𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀬𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑁂𑀲𑀁 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀦𑁂𑀳𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀺𑀧𑀸𑀲𑀸 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀍𑀆𑀳𑁄, 𑀲𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀲𑀼𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀳𑀻𑀦𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀲𑀼𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄, 𑀑𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀘𑁂𑀧𑀺 𑀢𑁂 𑀪𑁄𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀢𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸 𑀔𑀭𑀸 𑀓𑀝𑀼𑀓𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀪𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀯 𑀢𑁂 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀸𑀬 𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬. 𑀦𑁄 𑀘𑁂𑀧𑀺 𑀢𑁂 𑀪𑁄𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀑𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀢𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸 𑀔𑀭𑀸 𑀓𑀝𑀼𑀓𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀪𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀯 𑀢𑁂 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀸𑀬 𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬. 𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀢𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀫𑀸 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀪𑀸𑀲𑀺 𑀅𑀦𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀢𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸. 𑀇𑀫𑀸 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀢𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁄 𑀉𑀧𑀫𑀸 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀪𑀁𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀦𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀢𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸.
</TabItem>

1243.1 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, kiṃkusalagavesī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyesamāno magadhesu anupubbena cārikaṃ caramāno yena uruvelā senānigamo tadavasariṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀①🆎(kiṃ+kusala+gavesī) 🚻⨀②🆎(na+uttara) 🚻⨀②🆎(santi+vara+pada) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(pariyesamāna) 🚹⨂⑦(magadha) 🚻⨀③(anupubba) 🚺⨀②(cārikā) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(caramāna) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚺⨀①(uruvelā) 🚹⨀①🆎(senā+nigama) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(ta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(avasarati)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | seeking what is wholesome | unsurpassed | state of supreme peace | searching for | through Magadha | by stages | a journey | wandering | by where | Uruvelā | the army township | there | I arrived  
So, Aggivessana, I, seeking what is wholesome, searching for the unsurpassed state of supreme peace, wandering by stages through Magadha, arrived at Uruvelā, the army township.

1243.2 Tatthaddasaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ bhūmibhāgaṃ, pāsādikañca vanasaṇḍaṃ, nadiñca sandantiṃ setakaṃ supatitthaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ, samantā ca gocaragāmaṃ.  
⏏️(tattha) 🟢⨀👆⏮(passati) 🚹⨀②🔽(anīya)(ramaṇīya) 🚹⨀②🆎(bhūmi+bhāga) 🚹⨀②(pāsādika) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀②🆎(vana+saṇḍa) 🚺⨀②(nadī) ⏏️(ca) 🚺⨀②🔽(anta)(sandanta) 🚺⨀②(setaka) 🚺⨀②(supatittha) 🚺⨀②🔽(anīya)(ramaṇīya) ⏏️(samantā) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀②🆎(gocara+gāma)  
there | I saw | delightful | stretch of land | beautiful | and | grove | river | and | flowing | clear | with pleasant banks | delightful | around | and | a village for alms  
There I saw a delightful stretch of land, a beautiful grove, a clear-flowing river with pleasant banks, and a village for alms around it.

1243.3 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘ramaṇīyo vata bho bhūmibhāgo, pāsādiko ca vanasaṇḍo, nadī ca sandati setakā supatitthā ramaṇīyā, samantā ca gocaragāmo.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🚹⨀①🔽(anīya)(ramaṇīya) ⏏️(vata) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀①🆎(bhūmi+bhāga) 🚹⨀①(pāsādika) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀①🆎(vana+saṇḍa) 🚺⨀①(nadī) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(sandati) 🚺⨀①(setaka) 🚺⨀①(supatittha) 🚺⨀①🔽(anīya)(ramaṇīya) ⏏️(samantā) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀①🆎(gocara+gāma)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | was | delightful | indeed | Bho | stretch of land | beautiful | and | grove | river | and | flows | clear | with pleasant banks | delightful | around | and | a village for alms  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘Indeed, delightful is this stretch of land, beautiful is this grove, clear-flowing is this river with pleasant banks, and a village for alms is around it.

1243.4 Alaṃ vatidaṃ kulaputtassa padhānatthikassa padhānāyā’ti.  
⏏️(alaṃ) ⏏️(vata) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀④🆎(kula+putta) 🚹⨀④🆎(padhāna+atthika) 🚻⨀④(padhāna) ⏏️(iti)  
suitable | indeed | this | for a young man of good family | intent on striving | for striving | (end quote)  
This is indeed suitable for a young man of good family intent on striving.’

1243.5 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, tattheva nisīdiṃ ‘alamidaṃ padhānāyā’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) ⏏️(tattha) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀👆⏮(nisīdati) ⏏️(alaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ima) 🚻⨀④(padhāna) ⏏️(iti)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | there | right | I sat down | enough | this | for striving | (end quote)  
So, Aggivessana, I sat down right there, thinking, ‘This is enough for striving.’

1244.1 Apissumaṃ, aggivessana, tisso upamā paṭibhaṃsu anacchariyā pubbe assutapubbā.  
⏏️(api) ⏏️(ssu) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨂①(ti) 🚺⨂①(upamā) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(paṭibhāti) 🚺⨂①🆎(na+acchariya) ⏏️(pubbe) 🚺⨂①🆎(na+suta+pubba)  
and | then | to me | Aggivessana | three | similes | occurred | wonderful | before | never heard before  
Then, Aggivessana, three similes occurred to me, unheard-before and wonderful.

1244.2 Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, allaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnehaṃ udake nikkhittaṃ.  
⏏️(seyyathāpi) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚻⨀①(alla) 🚻⨀①(kaṭṭha) 🚻⨀①(sasneha) 🚻⨀⑦(udaka) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(nikkhitta)  
just as | Aggivessana | wet | log | sappy | in water | placed  
Just as, Aggivessana, if there were a wet sappy log placed in water,

1244.3 Atha puriso āgaccheyya uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya—  ‘aggiṃ abhinibbattessāmi, tejo pātukarissāmī’ti.  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①(purisa) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(āgacchati) 🚺⨀②🆎(uttara+araṇi) 🔽(ya)(ādāti) 🚹⨀②(aggi) 🟠⨀👆⏭(abhinibbatteti) 🚹⨀②(tejo) 🟢⨀👆⏭(pātukaroti) ⏏️(iti)  
then | a man | would come | an upper fire-stick | taking | a fire | I will produce | heat | I will make appear | (end quote)  
and a man came with an upper fire-stick, thinking: ‘I will light a fire, I will make heat appear.’

1244.4 Taṃ kiṃ maññasi, aggivessana, api nu so puriso amuṃ allaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnehaṃ, udake nikkhittaṃ, uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya abhimanthento aggiṃ abhinibbatteyya, tejo pātukareyyā”ti?  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(ta) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(kiṃ) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(maññati) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(nu) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) 🚹⨀①(purisa) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(amu) 🚻⨀②(alla) 🚻⨀②(kaṭṭha) 🚻⨀②(sasneha) 🚻⨀⑦(udaka) 🚻⨀②🔽(ta)(nikkhitta) 🚺⨀②🆎(uttara+araṇi) 🔽(ya)(ādāti) 🚹⨀①🔽(anta)(abhimanthenta) 🚹⨀②(aggi) 🟠⨀🤟⏯(abhinibbatteti) 🚹⨀②(tejo) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(pātukaroti) ⏏️(iti)  
that | what | do you think | Aggivessana | perhaps | (question) | that | man | that | wet | log | sappy | in water | placed | the upper fire-stick | taking | rubbing | a fire | could produce | heat | could make appear | (end quote)  
Aggivessana, what do you consider: could that man, by rubbing the wet sappy log placed in water with the upper fire-stick, light a fire and make heat appear?”

1244.5 “No hidaṃ, bho gotama”.  
⏏️(no) ⏏️(hi) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⓪(gotama)  
no | indeed | this | Bho | Gotama  
“Of course not, Bho Gotama.”

1244.6 “Taṃ kissa hetu”?  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ta) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀⑥(kiṃ) 🚹⨀①(hetu)  
that | of what | the reason  
“For what reason?”

1244.7 “Aduñhi, bho gotama, allaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnehaṃ, tañca pana udake nikkhittaṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(amu) ⏏️(hi) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⓪(gotama) 🚻⨀①(alla) 🚻⨀①(kaṭṭha) 🚻⨀①(sasneha) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ta) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(pana) 🚻⨀⑦(udaka) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(nikkhitta)  
that | because | Bho | Gotama | wet | log | sappy | it | and | moreover | in water | placed  
“Because, Bho Gotama, that log is wet and sappy, and moreover it is placed in water.

1244.8 Yāvadeva ca pana so puriso kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā”ti.  
⏏️(yāva) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(pana) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) 🚹⨀①(purisa) 🚹⨀⑥(kilamatha) 🚹⨀⑥(vighāta) 🚹⨀①(bhāgī) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(atthi) ⏏️(iti)  
as far as | only | and | moreover | that | man | of weariness | of vexation | a partaker | would be | (end quote)  
That man would only reap weariness and vexation.”

1244.9 “Evameva kho, aggivessana, ye hi keci samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā kāyena ceva cittena ca kāmehi avūpakaṭṭhā viharanti, yo ca nesaṃ kāmesu kāmacchando kāmasneho kāmamucchā kāmapipāsā kāmapariḷāho, so ca ajjhattaṃ na suppahīno hoti, na suppaṭippassaddho, opakkamikā cepi te bhonto samaṇabrāhmaṇā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayanti, abhabbāva te ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambodhāya.  
⏏️(evaṃ) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ya) ⏏️(hi) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(kaci) 🚹⨂①(samaṇa) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨂①(brāhmaṇa) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀③(kāya) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀③(citta) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨂⑤(kāma) 🚹⨂①🔽(ta)🆎(na+vūpakaṭṭha) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(viharati) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ya) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂⑥(ta) 🚹⨂⑦(kāma) 🚹⨀①🆎(kāma+chanda) 🚹⨀①🆎(kāma+sneha) 🚺⨀①🆎(kāma+mucchā) 🚺⨀①🆎(kāma+pipāsā) 🚹⨀①🆎(kāma+pariḷāha) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(ca) 🚻⨀②(ajjhatta) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀①🆎(su+pahīna) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀①🆎(su+paṭippassaddha) 🚺⨂②(opakkamika) ⏏️(ce) ⏏️(api) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ta) 🚹⨂①(bhavant) 🚹⨂①🆎(samaṇa+brāhmaṇa) 🚺⨂②(dukkha) 🚺⨂②🔽(ta)(tibba) 🚺⨂②(khara) 🚺⨂②(kaṭuka) 🚺⨂②(vedanā) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(vedayati) 🚹⨂①🔽(ṇya)🆎(na+bhabba) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ta) 🚻⨀④(ñāṇa) 🚻⨀④(dassana) 🚹⨀④🆎(na+uttara) 🚹⨀④(sambodha)  
in the same way | just | indeed | Aggivessana | whatever | indeed | any | wanderers | or | brahmins | or | in body | and | just | in mind | and | from sensual pleasures | not withdrawn | dwell | which | and | their | in sensual pleasures | desire for sensual pleasures | affection for sensual pleasures | infatuation with sensual pleasures | thirst for sensual pleasures | fever for sensual pleasures | that | and | internally | not | fully given up | is | not | fully stilled | due to exertion | if | even | those | worthy | religious practitioners | painful | sharp | harsh | bitter | feelings | feel | incapable | just | they | of knowledge | of vision | of unsurpassed | of full awakening  
“Exactly in the same way, Aggivessana, whatever `samaṇa` (wanderers) or brahmins dwell not withdrawn from sensual pleasures in body and mind, and their internal desire for sensual pleasures, affection for sensual pleasures, infatuation with sensual pleasures, thirst for sensual pleasures, fever for sensual pleasures is not fully given up and fully stilled — even if those worthy religious practitioners feel painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, they are incapable of knowledge and vision and unsurpassed full awakening.

1244.10 No cepi te bhonto samaṇabrāhmaṇā opakkamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayanti, abhabbāva te ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambodhāya.  
⏏️(no) ⏏️(ce) ⏏️(api) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ta) 🚹⨂①(bhavant) 🚹⨂①🆎(samaṇa+brāhmaṇa) 🚺⨂②(opakkamika) 🚺⨂②(dukkha) 🚺⨂②🔽(ta)(tibba) 🚺⨂②(khara) 🚺⨂②(kaṭuka) 🚺⨂②(vedanā) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(vedayati) 🚹⨂①🔽(ṇya)🆎(na+bhabba) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ta) 🚻⨀④(ñāṇa) 🚻⨀④(dassana) 🚹⨀④🆎(na+uttara) 🚹⨀④(sambodha)  
not | if | even | those | worthy | religious practitioners | due to exertion | painful | sharp | harsh | bitter | feelings | feel | incapable | just | they | of knowledge | of vision | of unsurpassed | of full awakening  
And even if those worthy religious practitioners do not feel painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, they are incapable of knowledge and vision and unsurpassed full awakening.

1244.11 Ayaṃ kho maṃ, aggivessana, paṭhamā upamā paṭibhāsi anacchariyā pubbe assutapubbā.  
*️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(ima) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨀①(paṭhama) 🚺⨀①(upamā) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(paṭibhāti) 🚺⨀①🆎(na+acchariya) ⏏️(pubbe) 🚺⨀①🆎(na+suta+pubba)  
this | indeed | to me | Aggivessana | first | simile | occurred | wonderful | before | never heard before  
This, Aggivessana, was the first simile that occurred to me, unheard-before and wonderful.

1245.1 Aparāpi kho maṃ, aggivessana, dutiyā upamā paṭibhāsi anacchariyā pubbe assutapubbā.  
*️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(apara) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨀①(dutiya) 🚺⨀①(upamā) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(paṭibhāti) 🚺⨀①🆎(na+acchariya) ⏏️(pubbe) 🚺⨀①🆎(na+suta+pubba)  
another | also | indeed | to me | Aggivessana | second | simile | occurred | wonderful | before | never heard before  
Then, Aggivessana, a second simile occurred to me, unheard-before and wonderful.

1245.2 Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, allaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnehaṃ, ārakā udakā thale nikkhittaṃ.  
⏏️(seyyathāpi) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚻⨀①(alla) 🚻⨀①(kaṭṭha) 🚻⨀①(sasneha) ⏏️(ārakā) 🚻⨀⑤(udaka) 🚻⨀⑦(thala) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(nikkhitta)  
just as | Aggivessana | wet | log | sappy | far | from water | on dry land | placed  
Just as, Aggivessana, if there were a wet sappy log placed on dry land far from water.

1245.3 Atha puriso āgaccheyya uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya—  ‘aggiṃ abhinibbattessāmi, tejo pātukarissāmī’ti.  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①(purisa) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(āgacchati) 🚺⨀②🆎(uttara+araṇi) 🔽(ya)(ādāti) 🚹⨀②(aggi) 🟠⨀👆⏭(abhinibbatteti) 🚹⨀②(tejo) 🟢⨀👆⏭(pātukaroti) ⏏️(iti)  
then | a man | would come | an upper fire-stick | taking | a fire | I will produce | heat | I will make appear | (end quote)  
And a man came with an upper fire-stick, thinking: ‘I will light a fire, I will make heat appear.’

1245.4 Taṃ kiṃ maññasi, aggivessana, api nu so puriso amuṃ allaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnehaṃ, ārakā udakā thale nikkhittaṃ, uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya abhimanthento aggiṃ abhinibbatteyya tejo pātukareyyā”ti?  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(ta) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(kiṃ) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(maññati) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(nu) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) 🚹⨀①(purisa) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(amu) 🚻⨀②(alla) 🚻⨀②(kaṭṭha) 🚻⨀②(sasneha) ⏏️(ārakā) 🚻⨀⑤(udaka) 🚻⨀⑦(thala) 🚻⨀②🔽(ta)(nikkhitta) 🚺⨀②🆎(uttara+araṇi) 🔽(ya)(ādāti) 🚹⨀①🔽(anta)(abhimanthenta) 🚹⨀②(aggi) 🟠⨀🤟⏯(abhinibbatteti) 🚹⨀②(tejo) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(pātukaroti) ⏏️(iti)  
that | what | do you think | Aggivessana | perhaps | (question) | that | man | that | wet | log | sappy | far | from water | on dry land | placed | the upper fire-stick | taking | rubbing | a fire | could produce | heat | could make appear | (end quote)  
Aggivessana, what do you consider: could that man, by rubbing the wet sappy log placed on dry land far from water with the upper fire-stick, light a fire and make heat appear?”

1245.5 “No hidaṃ, bho gotama”.  
⏏️(no) ⏏️(hi) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⓪(gotama)  
no | indeed | this | Bho | Gotama  
“Of course not, Bho Gotama.”

1245.6 “Taṃ kissa hetu”?  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ta) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀⑥(kiṃ) 🚹⨀①(hetu)  
that | of what | the reason  
“For what reason?”

1245.7 “Aduñhi, bho gotama, allaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnehaṃ, kiñcāpi ārakā udakā thale nikkhittaṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(amu) ⏏️(hi) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⓪(gotama) 🚻⨀①(alla) 🚻⨀①(kaṭṭha) 🚻⨀①(sasneha) ⏏️(kiñcāpi) ⏏️(ārakā) 🚻⨀⑤(udaka) 🚻⨀⑦(thala) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(nikkhitta)  
that | because | Bho | Gotama | wet | log | sappy | even though | far | from water | on dry land | placed  
“Because, Bho Gotama, that log is wet and sappy, even though it is placed on dry land far from water.

1245.8 Yāvadeva ca pana so puriso kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā”ti.  
⏏️(yāva) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(pana) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) 🚹⨀①(purisa) 🚹⨀⑥(kilamatha) 🚹⨀⑥(vighāta) 🚹⨀①(bhāgī) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(atthi) ⏏️(iti)  
as far as | only | and | moreover | that | man | of weariness | of vexation | a partaker | would be | (end quote)  
That man would only reap weariness and vexation.”

1245.9 “Evameva kho, aggivessana, ye hi keci samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā kāyena ceva cittena ca kāmehi vūpakaṭṭhā viharanti, yo ca nesaṃ kāmesu kāmacchando kāmasneho kāmamucchā kāmapipāsā kāmapariḷāho so ca ajjhattaṃ na suppahīno hoti, na suppaṭippassaddho, opakkamikā cepi te bhonto samaṇabrāhmaṇā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayanti, abhabbāva te ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambodhāya.

in the same way | just | indeed | Aggivessana | whatever | indeed | any | wanderers | or | brahmins | or | in body | and | just | in mind | and | from sensual pleasures | withdrawn | dwell | which | and | their | in sensual pleasures | desire for sensual pleasures | affection for sensual pleasures | infatuation with sensual pleasures | thirst for sensual pleasures | fever for sensual pleasures | that | and | internally | not | fully given up | is | not | fully stilled | due to exertion | if | even | those | worthy | religious practitioners | painful | sharp | harsh | bitter | feelings | feel | incapable | just | they | of knowledge | of vision | of unsurpassed | of full awakening  
“Exactly in the same way, Aggivessana, whatever wanderers or brahmins dwell withdrawn from sensual pleasures in body and mind, but their internal desire for sensual pleasures, affection for sensual pleasures, infatuation with sensual pleasures, thirst for sensual pleasures, fever for sensual pleasures is not fully given up and fully stilled — even if those worthy religious practitioners feel painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, they are incapable of knowledge and vision and unsurpassed full awakening.

And even if those worthy religious practitioners do not feel painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, they are incapable of knowledge and vision and unsurpassed full awakening.

## Breath control

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
1247\. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘What if I, clenching my teeth, pressing my tongue against the roof of my mouth, were to restrain, crush down, and torment mind with mind?’

So, Aggivessana, clenching my teeth, pressing my tongue against the roof of my mouth, I restrained, crushed down, and tormented mind with mind. While I was clenching my teeth, pressing my tongue against the roof of my mouth, and restraining, crushing down, and tormenting mind with mind, sweat ran from my armpits. Just as, Aggivessana, a strong man, seizing a weaker man by the head or shoulders, might restrain him, crush him down, and torment him; exactly so, Aggivessana, while I was clenching my teeth, pressing my tongue against the roof of my mouth, and restraining, crushing down, and tormenting mind with mind, sweat ran from my armpits. My energy was aroused and unsluggish, my awareness was established and unconfused, but my body was overwrought and uncalmed because I was overpowered by the pain of striving. Even such painful feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

1248\. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘What if I were to practise just the breathless `jhāna` (mental disposition)?’

So, Aggivessana, I stopped the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth and nose. When the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth and nose were stopped, there was a loud sound of winds coming out of my ear-holes. Just as there is a loud sound when a smith’s bellows are blown; exactly so, Aggivessana, when the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth and nose were stopped, there was a loud sound of winds coming out of my ear-holes. My energy was aroused and unsluggish, my awareness was established and unconfused, but my body was overwrought and uncalmed because I was overpowered by the pain of striving. Even such painful feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

1249\. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘What if I were to practise just the breathless mental disposition?’

So, Aggivessana, I stopped the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears. When the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears were stopped, violent winds cut through my head. Just as, Aggivessana, if a strong man were to crush one’s head with a sharp sword point; exactly so, Aggivessana, when the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears were stopped, violent winds cut through my head. My energy was aroused and unsluggish, my awareness was established and unconfused, but my body was overwrought and uncalmed because I was overpowered by the pain of striving. Even such painful feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

1250\. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘What if I were to practise just the breathless mental disposition?’

So, Aggivessana, I stopped the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears. When the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears were stopped, there were violent pains in my head. Just as, Aggivessana, if a strong man were to tighten a tough leather strap around one’s head; exactly so, Aggivessana, when the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears were stopped, there were violent pains in my head. My energy was aroused and unsluggish, my awareness was established and unconfused, but my body was overwrought and uncalmed because I was overpowered by the pain of striving. Even such painful feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

1251\. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘What if I were to practise just the breathless mental disposition?’

So, Aggivessana, I stopped the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears. When the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears were stopped, violent winds cut through my belly. Just as, Aggivessana, if a skilled butcher or his apprentice were to cut through a belly with a sharp butcher's knife; exactly so, Aggivessana, when the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears were stopped, violent winds cut through my belly. My energy was aroused and unsluggish, my awareness was established and unconfused, but my body was overwrought and uncalmed because I was overpowered by the pain of striving. Even such painful feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

1252\. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘What if I were to practise just the breathless mental disposition?’

So, Aggivessana, I stopped the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears. When the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears were stopped, there was a violent burning in my body. Just as, Aggivessana, if two strong men were to seize a weaker man by both arms and scorch and roast him over a pit of glowing coals; exactly so, Aggivessana, when the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears were stopped, there was a violent burning in my body. My energy was aroused and unsluggish, my awareness was established and unconfused, but my body was overwrought and uncalmed because I was overpowered by the pain of striving. Even such painful feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain. And then, Aggivessana, devas saw me and said:

> ‘The wanderer Gotama is dead.’

Some devas said:

> ‘The wanderer Gotama is not dead, but he is dying.’

Some devas said:

> ‘The wanderer Gotama is not dead, nor is he dying; the wanderer Gotama is an `arahant` (liberated one), such is the abiding of a liberated one.’
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
1247\. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ dantebhi dantamādhāya, jivhāya tāluṃ āhacca, cetasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇheyyaṃ abhinippīḷeyyaṃ abhisantāpeyyan’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, dantebhi dantamādhāya, jivhāya tāluṃ āhacca, cetasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhāmi abhinippīḷemi abhisantāpemi. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, dantebhi dantamādhāya jivhāya tāluṃ āhacca cetasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhato abhinippīḷayato abhisantāpayato kacchehi sedā muccanti. Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, balavā puriso dubbalataraṃ purisaṃ sīse vā gahetvā khandhe vā gahetvā abhiniggaṇheyya abhinippīḷeyya abhisantāpeyya; evameva kho me, aggivessana, dantebhi dantamādhāya, jivhāya tāluṃ āhacca, cetasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhato abhinippīḷayato abhisantāpayato kacchehi sedā muccanti. Āraddhaṃ kho pana me, aggivessana, vīriyaṃ hoti asallīnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, sāraddho ca pana me kāyo hoti appaṭippassaddho teneva dukkhappadhānena padhānābhitunnassa sato. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā dukkhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.

1248\. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ appāṇakaṃyeva jhānaṃ jhāyeyyan’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca assāsapassāse uparundhiṃ. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca assāsapassāsesu uparuddhesu kaṇṇasotehi vātānaṃ nikkhamantānaṃ adhimatto saddo hoti. Seyyathāpi nāma kammāragaggariyā dhamamānāya adhimatto saddo hoti; evameva kho me, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca assāsapassāsesu uparuddhesu kaṇṇasotehi vātānaṃ nikkhamantānaṃ adhimatto saddo hoti. Āraddhaṃ kho pana me, aggivessana, vīriyaṃ hoti asallīnaṃ upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā. Sāraddho ca pana me kāyo hoti appaṭippassaddho teneva dukkhappadhānena padhānābhitunnassa sato. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā dukkhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.

1249\. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ appāṇakaṃyeva jhānaṃ jhāyeyyan’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca kaṇṇato ca assāsapassāse uparundhiṃ. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca kaṇṇato ca assāsapassāsesu uparuddhesu adhimattā vātā muddhani ūhananti. Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, balavā puriso tiṇhena sikharena muddhani abhimattheyya; evameva kho me, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca kaṇṇato ca assāsapassāsesu uparuddhesu adhimattā vātā muddhani ūhananti. Āraddhaṃ kho pana me, aggivessana, vīriyaṃ hoti asallīnaṃ upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā. Sāraddho ca pana me kāyo hoti appaṭippassaddho teneva dukkhappadhānena padhānābhitunnassa sato. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā dukkhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.

1250\. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ appāṇakaṃyeva jhānaṃ jhāyeyyan’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca kaṇṇato ca assāsapassāse uparundhiṃ. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca kaṇṇato ca assāsapassāsesu uparuddhesu adhimattā sīse sīsavedanā honti. Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, balavā puriso daḷhena varattakkhaṇḍena sīse sīsaveṭhaṃ dadeyya; evameva kho me, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca kaṇṇato ca assāsapassāsesu uparuddhesu adhimattā sīse sīsavedanā honti. Āraddhaṃ kho pana me, aggivessana, vīriyaṃ hoti asallīnaṃ upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā. Sāraddho ca pana me kāyo hoti appaṭippassaddho teneva dukkhappadhānena padhānābhitunnassa sato. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā dukkhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.

1251\. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ appāṇakaṃyeva jhānaṃ jhāyeyyan’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca kaṇṇato ca assāsapassāse uparundhiṃ. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca kaṇṇato ca assāsapassāsesu uparuddhesu adhimattā vātā kucchiṃ parikantanti. Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, dakkho goghātako vā goghātakantevāsī vā tiṇhena govikantanena kucchiṃ parikanteyya; evameva kho me, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca kaṇṇato ca assāsapassāsesu uparuddhesu adhimattā vātā kucchiṃ parikantanti. Āraddhaṃ kho pana me, aggivessana, vīriyaṃ hoti asallīnaṃ upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā. Sāraddho ca pana me kāyo hoti appaṭippassaddho teneva dukkhappadhānena padhānābhitunnassa sato. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā dukkhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.

1252\. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ appāṇakaṃyeva jhānaṃ jhāyeyyan’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca kaṇṇato ca assāsapassāse uparundhiṃ. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca kaṇṇato ca assāsapassāsesu uparuddhesu adhimatto kāyasmiṃ ḍāho hoti. Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, dve balavanto purisā dubbalataraṃ purisaṃ nānābāhāsu gahetvā aṅgārakāsuyā santāpeyyuṃ samparitāpeyyuṃ; evameva kho me, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca kaṇṇato ca assāsapassāsesu uparuddhesu adhimatto kāyasmiṃ ḍāho hoti. Āraddhaṃ kho pana me, aggivessana, vīriyaṃ hoti asallīnaṃ upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā. Sāraddho ca pana me kāyo hoti appaṭippassaddho teneva dukkhappadhānena padhānābhitunnassa sato. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā dukkhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati. Apissu maṃ, aggivessana, devatā disvā evamāhaṃsu—  ‘kālaṅkato samaṇo gotamo’ti. Ekaccā devatā evamāhaṃsu—  ‘na kālaṅkato samaṇo gotamo, api ca kālaṃ karotī’ti. Ekaccā devatā evamāhaṃsu—  ‘na kālaṅkato samaṇo gotamo, napi kālaṃ karoti, arahaṃ samaṇo gotamo, vihāro tveva so arahato evarūpo hotī’ti.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
1247\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀤𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀪𑀺 𑀤𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀫𑀸𑀥𑀸𑀬, 𑀚𑀺𑀯𑁆𑀳𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀸𑀮𑀼𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀘𑁆𑀘, 𑀘𑁂𑀢𑀲𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀡𑁆𑀳𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀻𑀍𑀏𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀧𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀤𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀪𑀺 𑀤𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀫𑀸𑀥𑀸𑀬, 𑀚𑀺𑀯𑁆𑀳𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀸𑀮𑀼𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀘𑁆𑀘, 𑀘𑁂𑀢𑀲𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀡𑁆𑀳𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀻𑀍𑀏𑀫𑀺 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀧𑁂𑀫𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀤𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀪𑀺 𑀤𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀫𑀸𑀥𑀸𑀬 𑀚𑀺𑀯𑁆𑀳𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀸𑀮𑀼𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀘𑁆𑀘 𑀘𑁂𑀢𑀲𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀡𑁆𑀳𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀻𑀍𑀅𑀬𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀧𑀬𑀢𑁄 𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀙𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀲𑁂𑀤𑀸 𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀩𑀮𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀤𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀮𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀁 𑀲𑀻𑀲𑁂 𑀯𑀸 𑀕𑀳𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀔𑀦𑁆𑀥𑁂 𑀯𑀸 𑀕𑀳𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀡𑁆𑀳𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀻𑀍𑀏𑀬𑁆𑀬 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀧𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀤𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀪𑀺 𑀤𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀫𑀸𑀥𑀸𑀬, 𑀚𑀺𑀯𑁆𑀳𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀸𑀮𑀼𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀘𑁆𑀘, 𑀘𑁂𑀢𑀲𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀡𑁆𑀳𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀻𑀍𑀅𑀬𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀧𑀬𑀢𑁄 𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀙𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀲𑁂𑀤𑀸 𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺. 𑀆𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀯𑀻𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀻𑀦𑀁, 𑀉𑀧𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀢𑀸 𑀲𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸, 𑀲𑀸𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑁂𑀯 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀪𑀺𑀢𑀼𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀲𑀢𑁄. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺.

1248\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀡𑀓𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀛𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀛𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀦𑁆𑀥𑀺𑀁. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀲𑁄𑀢𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀯𑀸𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀤𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀦𑀸𑀫 𑀓𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀭𑀕𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀥𑀫𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀤𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀲𑁄𑀢𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀯𑀸𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀤𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺. 𑀆𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀯𑀻𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀻𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀢𑀸 𑀲𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸. 𑀲𑀸𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑁂𑀯 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀪𑀺𑀢𑀼𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀲𑀢𑁄. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺.

1249\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀡𑀓𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀛𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀛𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀦𑁆𑀥𑀺𑀁. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀯𑀸𑀢𑀸 𑀫𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀦𑀺 𑀊𑀳𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀩𑀮𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀢𑀺𑀡𑁆𑀳𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀺𑀔𑀭𑁂𑀦 𑀫𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀦𑀺 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀣𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀯𑀸𑀢𑀸 𑀫𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀦𑀺 𑀊𑀳𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺. 𑀆𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀯𑀻𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀻𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀢𑀸 𑀲𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸. 𑀲𑀸𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑁂𑀯 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀪𑀺𑀢𑀼𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀲𑀢𑁄. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺.

1250\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀡𑀓𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀛𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀛𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀦𑁆𑀥𑀺𑀁. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀲𑀻𑀲𑁂 𑀲𑀻𑀲𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀳𑁄𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀩𑀮𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀤𑀍𑀳𑁂𑀦 𑀯𑀭𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀡𑁆𑀟𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀻𑀲𑁂 𑀲𑀻𑀲𑀯𑁂𑀞𑀁 𑀤𑀤𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀲𑀻𑀲𑁂 𑀲𑀻𑀲𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀳𑁄𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺. 𑀆𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀯𑀻𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀻𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀢𑀸 𑀲𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸. 𑀲𑀸𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑁂𑀯 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀪𑀺𑀢𑀼𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀲𑀢𑁄. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺.

1251\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀡𑀓𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀛𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀛𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀦𑁆𑀥𑀺𑀁. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀯𑀸𑀢𑀸 𑀓𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀤𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀖𑀸𑀢𑀓𑁄 𑀯𑀸 𑀕𑁄𑀖𑀸𑀢𑀓𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀯𑀸𑀲𑀻 𑀯𑀸 𑀢𑀺𑀡𑁆𑀳𑁂𑀦 𑀕𑁄𑀯𑀺𑀓𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀦𑁂𑀦 𑀓𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀯𑀸𑀢𑀸 𑀓𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺. 𑀆𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀯𑀻𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀻𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀢𑀸 𑀲𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸. 𑀲𑀸𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑁂𑀯 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀪𑀺𑀢𑀼𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀲𑀢𑁄. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺.

1252\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀡𑀓𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀛𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀛𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀦𑁆𑀥𑀺𑀁. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀟𑀸𑀳𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀤𑁆𑀯𑁂 𑀩𑀮𑀯𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀮𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀁 𑀦𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀸𑀲𑀼 𑀕𑀳𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀗𑁆𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀓𑀸𑀲𑀼𑀬𑀸 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀧𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀼𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀢𑀸𑀧𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀼𑀁; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀉𑀧𑀭𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀟𑀸𑀳𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺. 𑀆𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀯𑀻𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀻𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀢𑀸 𑀲𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸. 𑀲𑀸𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑁂𑀯 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀪𑀺𑀢𑀼𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀲𑀢𑁄. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺. 𑀅𑀧𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀤𑁂𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑀸𑀳𑀁𑀲𑀼—  ‘𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄’𑀢𑀺. 𑀏𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀸 𑀤𑁂𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑀸𑀳𑀁𑀲𑀼—  ‘𑀦 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄, 𑀅𑀧𑀺 𑀘 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀁 𑀓𑀭𑁄𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺. 𑀏𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀸 𑀤𑁂𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑀸𑀳𑀁𑀲𑀼—  ‘𑀦 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄, 𑀦𑀧𑀺 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀁 𑀓𑀭𑁄𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀭𑀳𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄, 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀭𑁄 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑁂𑀯 𑀲𑁄 𑀅𑀭𑀳𑀢𑁄 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺.
</TabItem>
<TabItem label="Interlinear">
1247.1 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ dantebhi dantamādhāya, jivhāya tāluṃ āhacca, cetasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇheyyaṃ abhinippīḷeyyaṃ abhisantāpeyyan’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ya) ⏏️(nūna) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂③(danta) 🚹⨀②(danta) 🔽(ya)(ādahati) 🚺⨀③(jivhā) 🚻⨀②(tālu) 🔽(ya)(āhanati) 🚹⨀③(cetas) 🚻⨀②(citta) 🔵⨀👆⏯(abhiniggaṇhāti) 🔵⨀👆⏯(abhinippīḷeti) 🔵⨀👆⏯(abhisantāpeti) ⏏️(iti)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | occurred | what | if | I | with the teeth | the tooth | having placed | with the tongue | the palate | having pressed | with the mind | the mind | I would restrain | I would crush down | I would torment | (end quote)  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘What if I, clenching my teeth, pressing my tongue against the roof of my mouth, were to restrain, crush down, and torment mind with mind?’

1247.2 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, dantebhi dantamādhāya, jivhāya tāluṃ āhacca, cetasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhāmi abhinippīḷemi abhisantāpemi.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨂③(danta) 🚹⨀②(danta) 🔽(ya)(ādahati) 🚺⨀③(jivhā) 🚻⨀②(tālu) 🔽(ya)(āhanati) 🚹⨀③(cetas) 🚻⨀②(citta) 🟢⨀👆▶️(abhiniggaṇhāti) 🟢⨀👆▶️(abhinippīḷeti) 🟠⨀👆▶️(abhisantāpeti)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | with the teeth | the tooth | having placed | with the tongue | the palate | having pressed | with the mind | the mind | I restrain | I crush down | I torment  
So, Aggivessana, clenching my teeth, pressing my tongue against the roof of my mouth, I restrained, crushed down, and tormented mind with mind.

1247.3 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, dantebhi dantamādhāya jivhāya tāluṃ āhacca cetasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhato abhinippīḷayato abhisantāpayato kacchehi sedā muccanti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨂③(danta) 🚹⨀②(danta) 🔽(ya)(ādahati) 🚺⨀③(jivhā) 🚻⨀②(tālu) 🔽(ya)(āhanati) 🚹⨀③(cetas) 🚻⨀②(citta) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(anta)(abhiniggaṇhanta) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(anta)(abhinippīḷayanta) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(anta)(abhisantāpayanta) 🚹⨂⑤(kaccha) 🚹⨂①(seda) 🔴⨂🤟▶️(muccati)  
of that | of me | Aggivessana | with the teeth | the tooth | having placed | with the tongue | the palate | having pressed | with the mind | the mind | restraining | crushing down | tormenting | from the armpits | sweat | runs  
While I was clenching my teeth, pressing my tongue against the roof of my mouth, and restraining, crushing down, and tormenting mind with mind, sweat ran from my armpits.

1247.4 Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, balavā puriso dubbalataraṃ purisaṃ sīse vā gahetvā khandhe vā gahetvā abhiniggaṇheyya abhinippīḷeyya abhisantāpeyya;  
⏏️(seyyathāpi) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀①🔽(vantu)(balavant) 🚹⨀①(purisa) 🚹⨀②🔽(tara)(dubbalatara) 🚹⨀②(purisa) 🚻⨀⑦(sīsa) ⏏️(vā) 🔽(tvā)(gaṇhāti) 🚹⨀⑦(khandha) ⏏️(vā) 🔽(tvā)(gaṇhāti) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(abhiniggaṇhāti) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(abhinippīḷeti) 🟠⨀🤟⏯(abhisantāpeti)  
just as | Aggivessana | strong | man | weaker | man | by the head | or | having seized | by the shoulder | or | having seized | would restrain | would crush down | would torment  
Just as, Aggivessana, a strong man, seizing a weaker man by the head or shoulders, might restrain him, crush him down, and torment him;

1247.5 evameva kho me, aggivessana, dantebhi dantamādhāya, jivhāya tāluṃ āhacca, cetasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhato abhinippīḷayato abhisantāpayato kacchehi sedā muccanti.  
⏏️(evaṃ) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨂③(danta) 🚹⨀②(danta) 🔽(ya)(ādahati) 🚺⨀③(jivhā) 🚻⨀②(tālu) 🔽(ya)(āhanati) 🚹⨀③(cetas) 🚻⨀②(citta) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(anta)(abhiniggaṇhanta) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(anta)(abhinippīḷayanta) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(anta)(abhisantāpayanta) 🚹⨂⑤(kaccha) 🚹⨂①(seda) 🔴⨂🤟▶️(muccati)  
in the same way | just | indeed | of me | Aggivessana | with the teeth | the tooth | having placed | with the tongue | the palate | having pressed | with the mind | the mind | restraining | crushing down | tormenting | from the armpits | sweat | runs  
exactly so, Aggivessana, while I was clenching my teeth, pressing my tongue against the roof of my mouth, and restraining, crushing down, and tormenting mind with mind, sweat ran from my armpits.

1247.6 Āraddhaṃ kho pana me, aggivessana, vīriyaṃ hoti asallīnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, sāraddho ca pana me kāyo hoti appaṭippassaddho teneva dukkhappadhānena padhānābhitunnassa sato.

aroused | indeed | now | my | Aggivessana | energy | is | unsluggish | established | awareness | unconfused | overwrought | but | now | my | body | is | uncalmed | by that | very | painful striving | overpowered by striving | being  
My energy was aroused and unsluggish, my awareness was established and unconfused, but my body was overwrought and uncalmed because I was overpowered by the pain of striving.

1247.7 Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā dukkhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.  
🚺⨀①🆎(evaṃ+rūpa) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(uppanna) 🚺⨀①(dukkha) 🚺⨀①(vedanā) 🚻⨀②(citta) ⏏️(na) 🔽(ya)(pariyādiyati) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(tiṭṭhati)  
such | even | indeed | my | Aggivessana | arisen | painful | feeling | the mind | not | invading | remains  
Even such painful feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

1248.1 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ appāṇakaṃyeva jhānaṃ jhāyeyyan’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ya) ⏏️(nūna) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚻⨀②🔽(ka)🆎(na+pāṇa) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀②(jhāna) 🔵⨀👆⏯(jhāyati) ⏏️(iti)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | occurred | what | if | I | breathless | just | mental disposition | I would practise | (end quote)  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘What if I were to practise just the breathless `jhāna` (mental disposition)?’

1248.2 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca assāsapassāse uparundhiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚻⨀⑤(mukha) ⏏️(ca) 🚺⨀⑤(nāsā) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨂②🆎(assāsa+passāsa) 🟢⨀👆⏮(uparundhati)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | through the mouth | and | through the nose | and | the in-breaths and out-breaths | I stopped  
So, Aggivessana, I stopped the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth and nose.

1248.3 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, mukhato ca nāsato ca assāsapassāsesu uparuddhesu kaṇṇasotehi vātānaṃ nikkhamantānaṃ adhimatto saddo hoti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚻⨀⑤(mukha) ⏏️(ca) 🚺⨀⑤(nāsā) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨂⑦🆎(assāsa+passāsa) 🚹⨂⑦🔽(ta)(uparuddha) 🚻⨂⑤🆎(kaṇṇa+sota) 🚹⨂⑥(vāta) 🚹⨂⑥🔽(anta)(nikkhamanta) 🚹⨀①(adhimatta) 🚹⨀①(sadda) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti)  
of that | of me | Aggivessana | from the mouth | and | from the nose | and | the in-breaths and out-breaths | when stopped | from the ear-holes | of winds | coming out | intense | sound | there is  
When the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth and nose were stopped, there was a loud sound of winds coming out of my ear-holes.

1248.4 Seyyathāpi nāma kammāragaggariyā dhamamānāya adhimatto saddo hoti;  
⏏️(seyyathāpi) ⏏️(nāma) 🚺⨀⑥🆎(kammāra+gaggarī) 🚺⨀⑥🔽(māna)(dhamamāna) 🚹⨀①(adhimatta) 🚹⨀①(sadda) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti)

Just as, Aggivessana, if a strong man were to tighten a tough leather strap around one’s head;

exactly so, Aggivessana, when the in-breaths and out-breaths through my mouth, nose, and ears were stopped, there were violent pains in my head.

1250.6 Āraddhaṃ kho pana me, aggivessana, vīriyaṃ hoti asallīnaṃ upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā.  
🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(āraddha) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(pana) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚻⨀①(vīriya) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)🆎(na+sallīna) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(upaṭṭhita) 🚺⨀①(sati) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)🆎(na+sammuṭṭha)  
aroused | indeed | now | my | Aggivessana | energy | is | unsluggish | established | awareness | unconfused  
My energy was aroused and unsluggish, my awareness was established and unconfused,

1250.7 Sāraddho ca pana me kāyo hoti appaṭippassaddho teneva dukkhappadhānena padhānābhitunnassa sato.

overwrought | but | now | my | body | is | uncalmed | by that | very | painful striving | overpowered by striving | being  
but my body was overwrought and uncalmed because I was overpowered by the pain of striving.

1250.8 Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā dukkhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.  
🚺⨀①🆎(evaṃ+rūpa) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(uppanna) 🚺⨀①(dukkha) 🚺⨀①(vedanā) 🚻⨀②(citta) ⏏️(na) 🔽(ya)(pariyādiyati) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(tiṭṭhati)  
such | even | indeed | my | Aggivessana | arisen | painful | feeling | the mind | not | invading | remains  
Even such painful feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

1251.1 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ appāṇakaṃyeva jhānaṃ jhāyeyyan’ti.

1252.4 Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, dve balavanto purisā dubbalataraṃ purisaṃ nānābāhāsu gahetvā aṅgārakāsuyā santāpeyyuṃ samparitāpeyyuṃ;

Some devas said: ‘The wanderer Gotama is not dead, nor is he dying; the wanderer Gotama is an `arahant` (liberated one), such is the abiding of a liberated one.’
