## Fasting

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
1253\. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘What if I were to practise the complete cutting off of food?’

Then, Aggivessana, devas approached me and said:

> ‘Do not, Mārisa, practise the complete cutting off of food. If you, Mārisa, practise the complete cutting off of food, we will infuse divine nectar into your pores, and you will subsist on that.’

Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘If I claim to be completely fasting while these devas infuse divine nectar into my pores and I subsist on that, that would be a falsehood on my part.’

So, Aggivessana, I dismissed those devas, saying

> ‘Enough.’

1254\. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘What if I were to take very little food, a handful at a time, whether of mung bean soup, or horse gram soup, or chickpea soup, or pea soup?’

So, Aggivessana, I took very little food, a handful at a time, whether of mung bean soup, or horse gram soup, or chickpea soup, or pea soup. While I was taking very little food, a handful at a time, whether of mung bean soup, or horse gram soup, or chickpea soup, or pea soup, my body reached a state of extreme emaciation. Because of eating so little, my limbs became like the joints of a liana or of the necklace plant. Because of eating so little, my backside became like a camel’s hoof. Because of eating so little, my spine became raised and lowered like a string of beads. Because of eating so little, my ribs collapsed and hung askew like the rafters of an old shed. Because of eating so little, the pupils of my eyes appeared deep sunk in their sockets, like the sparkle of the water seen sunk deep in a deep well. Because of eating so little, my scalp became shrivelled and withered like a bitter gourd cut while raw and withered by the wind and sun.

1255\. If I thought ‘I will touch the skin of my belly,’ Aggivessana, I grabbed my spine. If I thought ‘I will touch my spine,’ I grabbed the skin of my belly. To such an extent, Aggivessana, did the skin of my belly adhere to my spine because of eating so little. If I thought ‘I will defecate or urinate,’ Aggivessana, I fell over on my face right there, because of eating so little. If I tried to ease this body by rubbing my limbs with my hand, Aggivessana, the hairs, rotted at their roots, fell from my body as I rubbed my limbs with my hand, because of eating so little. Then, Aggivessana, people saw me and said: ‘The wanderer Gotama is black.’ Some people said: ‘The wanderer Gotama is not black, the wanderer Gotama is brown.’ Some people said: ‘The wanderer Gotama is not black, nor brown, the wanderer Gotama has a sallow complexion.’ To such an extent, Aggivessana, was my pure and bright complexion ruined because of eating so little.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
1253\. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ sabbaso āhārupacchedāya paṭipajjeyyan’ti. Atha kho maṃ, aggivessana, devatā upasaṅkamitvā etadavocuṃ—  ‘mā kho tvaṃ, mārisa, sabbaso āhārupacchedāya paṭipajji. Sace kho tvaṃ, mārisa, sabbaso āhārupacchedāya paṭipajjissasi, tassa te mayaṃ dibbaṃ ojaṃ lomakūpehi ajjhohāressāma, tāya tvaṃ yāpessasī’ti. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘ahañceva kho pana sabbaso ajajjitaṃ paṭijāneyyaṃ, imā ca me devatā dibbaṃ ojaṃ lomakūpehi ajjhohāreyyuṃ, tāya cāhaṃ yāpeyyaṃ, taṃ mamassa musā’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, tā devatā paccācikkhāmi, ‘halan’ti vadāmi.

1254\. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ thokaṃ thokaṃ āhāraṃ āhāreyyaṃ, pasataṃ pasataṃ, yadi vā muggayūsaṃ, yadi vā kulatthayūsaṃ, yadi vā kaḷāyayūsaṃ, yadi vā hareṇukayūsan’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, thokaṃ thokaṃ āhāraṃ āhāresiṃ, pasataṃ pasataṃ, yadi vā muggayūsaṃ, yadi vā kulatthayūsaṃ, yadi vā kaḷāyayūsaṃ, yadi vā hareṇukayūsaṃ. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, thokaṃ thokaṃ āhāraṃ āhārayato, pasataṃ pasataṃ, yadi vā muggayūsaṃ, yadi vā kulatthayūsaṃ, yadi vā kaḷāyayūsaṃ, yadi vā hareṇukayūsaṃ, adhimattakasimānaṃ patto kāyo hoti. Seyyathāpi nāma āsītikapabbāni vā kāḷapabbāni vā; evamevassu me aṅgapaccaṅgāni bhavanti tāyevappāhāratāya. Seyyathāpi nāma oṭṭhapadaṃ; evamevassu me ānisadaṃ hoti tāyevappāhāratāya. Seyyathāpi nāma vaṭṭanāvaḷī; evamevassu me piṭṭhikaṇṭako uṇṇatāvanato hoti tāyevappāhāratāya. Seyyathāpi nāma jarasālāya gopānasiyo oluggaviluggā bhavanti; evamevassu me phāsuḷiyo oluggaviluggā bhavanti tāyevappāhāratāya. Seyyathāpi nāma gambhīre udapāne udakatārakā gambhīragatā okkhāyikā dissanti; evamevassu me akkhikūpesu akkhitārakā gambhīragatā okkhāyikā dissanti tāyevappāhāratāya. Seyyathāpi nāma tittakālābu āmakacchinno vātātapena samphuṭito hoti sammilāto; evamevassu me sīsacchavi samphuṭitā hoti sammilātā tāyevappāhāratāya.

1255\. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, udaracchaviṃ parimasissāmīti piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṃyeva pariggaṇhāmi, piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṃ parimasissāmīti udaracchaviṃyeva pariggaṇhāmi, yāvassu me, aggivessana, udaracchavi piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṃ allīnā hoti tāyevappāhāratāya. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, vaccaṃ vā muttaṃ vā karissāmīti tattheva avakujjo papatāmi tāyevappāhāratāya. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, imameva kāyaṃ assāsento pāṇinā gattāni anumajjāmi. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, pāṇinā gattāni anumajjato pūtimūlāni lomāni kāyasmā papatanti tāyevappāhāratāya. Apissu maṃ, aggivessana, manussā disvā evamāhaṃsu—  ‘kāḷo samaṇo gotamo’ti. Ekacce manussā evamāhaṃsu—  ‘na kāḷo samaṇo gotamo, sāmo samaṇo gotamo’ti. Ekacce manussā evamāhaṃsu—  ‘na kāḷo samaṇo gotamo, napi sāmo, maṅguracchavi samaṇo gotamo’ti. Yāvassu me, aggivessana, tāva parisuddho chavivaṇṇo pariyodāto upahato hoti tāyevappāhāratāya.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
1253\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀲𑁄 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀼𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀙𑁂𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀤𑁂𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀼𑀁—  ‘𑀫𑀸 𑀔𑁄 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁, 𑀫𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀲, 𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀲𑁄 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀼𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀙𑁂𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀺. 𑀲𑀘𑁂 𑀔𑁄 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁, 𑀫𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀲, 𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀲𑁄 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀼𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀙𑁂𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀲𑀺, 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀢𑁂 𑀫𑀬𑀁 𑀤𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀁 𑀑𑀚𑀁 𑀮𑁄𑀫𑀓𑀽𑀧𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑁄𑀳𑀸𑀭𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫, 𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀬𑀸𑀧𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀲𑀻’𑀢𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀅𑀳𑀜𑁆𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀲𑁄 𑀅𑀚𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀺𑀢𑀁 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀚𑀸𑀦𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁, 𑀇𑀫𑀸 𑀘 𑀫𑁂 𑀤𑁂𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀤𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀁 𑀑𑀚𑀁 𑀮𑁄𑀫𑀓𑀽𑀧𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑁄𑀳𑀸𑀭𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀼𑀁, 𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀘𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀬𑀸𑀧𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁, 𑀢𑀁 𑀫𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀼𑀲𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀢𑀸 𑀤𑁂𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀸𑀘𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸𑀫𑀺, ‘𑀳𑀮𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺 𑀯𑀤𑀸𑀫𑀺.

1254\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀣𑁄𑀓𑀁 𑀣𑁄𑀓𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁, 𑀧𑀲𑀢𑀁 𑀧𑀲𑀢𑀁, 𑀬𑀤𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀫𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀬𑀽𑀲𑀁, 𑀬𑀤𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀼𑀮𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀬𑀽𑀲𑀁, 𑀬𑀤𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀍𑀆𑀬𑀬𑀽𑀲𑀁, 𑀬𑀤𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀳𑀭𑁂𑀡𑀼𑀓𑀬𑀽𑀲𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀣𑁄𑀓𑀁 𑀣𑁄𑀓𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑁂𑀲𑀺𑀁, 𑀧𑀲𑀢𑀁 𑀧𑀲𑀢𑀁, 𑀬𑀤𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀫𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀬𑀽𑀲𑀁, 𑀬𑀤𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀼𑀮𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀬𑀽𑀲𑀁, 𑀬𑀤𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀍𑀆𑀬𑀬𑀽𑀲𑀁, 𑀬𑀤𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀳𑀭𑁂𑀡𑀼𑀓𑀬𑀽𑀲𑀁. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀣𑁄𑀓𑀁 𑀣𑁄𑀓𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀬𑀢𑁄, 𑀧𑀲𑀢𑀁 𑀧𑀲𑀢𑀁, 𑀬𑀤𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀫𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀬𑀽𑀲𑀁, 𑀬𑀤𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀼𑀮𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀬𑀽𑀲𑀁, 𑀬𑀤𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀍𑀆𑀬𑀬𑀽𑀲𑀁, 𑀬𑀤𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀳𑀭𑁂𑀡𑀼𑀓𑀬𑀽𑀲𑀁, 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀓𑀲𑀺𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀦𑀸𑀫 𑀆𑀲𑀻𑀢𑀺𑀓𑀧𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀸𑀍𑀅𑀧𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀯𑀸; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀫𑁂 𑀅𑀗𑁆𑀕𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀗𑁆𑀕𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀪𑀯𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺 𑀢𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀯𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀦𑀸𑀫 𑀑𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀧𑀤𑀁; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀦𑀺𑀲𑀤𑀁 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀢𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀯𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀦𑀸𑀫 𑀯𑀝𑁆𑀝𑀦𑀸𑀯𑀍𑀈; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀫𑁂 𑀧𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀝𑀓𑁄 𑀉𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀦𑀢𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀢𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀯𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀦𑀸𑀫 𑀚𑀭𑀲𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀬 𑀕𑁄𑀧𑀸𑀦𑀲𑀺𑀬𑁄 𑀑𑀮𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀯𑀺𑀮𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀸 𑀪𑀯𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀫𑁂 𑀨𑀸𑀲𑀼𑀍𑀇𑀬𑁄 𑀑𑀮𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀯𑀺𑀮𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀸 𑀪𑀯𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺 𑀢𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀯𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀦𑀸𑀫 𑀕𑀫𑁆𑀪𑀻𑀭𑁂 𑀉𑀤𑀧𑀸𑀦𑁂 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀢𑀸𑀭𑀓𑀸 𑀕𑀫𑁆𑀪𑀻𑀭𑀕𑀢𑀸 𑀑𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸𑀬𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀫𑁂 𑀅𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀓𑀽𑀧𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀢𑀸𑀭𑀓𑀸 𑀕𑀫𑁆𑀪𑀻𑀭𑀕𑀢𑀸 𑀑𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸𑀬𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺 𑀢𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀯𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀦𑀸𑀫 𑀢𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀩𑀼 𑀆𑀫𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀦𑁄 𑀯𑀸𑀢𑀸𑀢𑀧𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀨𑀼𑀝𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀮𑀸𑀢𑁄; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀫𑁂 𑀲𑀻𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀯𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀨𑀼𑀝𑀺𑀢𑀸 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀮𑀸𑀢𑀸 𑀢𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀯𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬.

1255\. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀤𑀭𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀯𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀫𑀲𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺 𑀧𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀝𑀓𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀡𑁆𑀳𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀧𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀝𑀓𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀫𑀲𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺 𑀉𑀤𑀭𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀯𑀺𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀡𑁆𑀳𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀬𑀸𑀯𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀤𑀭𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀯𑀺 𑀧𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀓𑀡𑁆𑀝𑀓𑀁 𑀅𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀻𑀦𑀸 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀢𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀯𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀯𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀁 𑀯𑀸 𑀫𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺 𑀢𑀢𑁆𑀣𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀯𑀓𑀼𑀚𑁆𑀚𑁄 𑀧𑀧𑀢𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀢𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀯𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀇𑀫𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀸𑀡𑀺𑀦𑀸 𑀕𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀫𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀸𑀫𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀧𑀸𑀡𑀺𑀦𑀸 𑀕𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀫𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀽𑀢𑀺𑀫𑀽𑀮𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀮𑁄𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀧𑀧𑀢𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺 𑀢𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀯𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬. 𑀅𑀧𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸 𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑀸𑀳𑀁𑀲𑀼—  ‘𑀓𑀸𑀍𑀑 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄’𑀢𑀺. 𑀏𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁂 𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑀸𑀳𑀁𑀲𑀼—  ‘𑀦 𑀓𑀸𑀍𑀑 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄, 𑀲𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄’𑀢𑀺. 𑀏𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁂 𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑀸𑀳𑀁𑀲𑀼—  ‘𑀦 𑀓𑀸𑀍𑀑 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄, 𑀦𑀧𑀺 𑀲𑀸𑀫𑁄, 𑀫𑀗𑁆𑀕𑀼𑀭𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀯𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄’𑀢𑀺. 𑀬𑀸𑀯𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀢𑀸𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀙𑀯𑀺𑀯𑀡𑁆𑀡𑁄 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀤𑀸𑀢𑁄 𑀉𑀧𑀳𑀢𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀢𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀯𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬.

1253.1 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ sabbaso āhārupacchedāya paṭipajjeyyan’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ya) ⏏️(nūna) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(sabbaso) 🚹⨀④🆎(āhāra+upaccheda) 🔵⨀👆⏯(paṭipajjati) ⏏️(iti)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | occurred | what | if | I | completely | for the cutting off of food | I should practise | (end quote)  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘What if I were to practise the complete cutting off of food?’

1253.2 Atha kho maṃ, aggivessana, devatā upasaṅkamitvā etadavocuṃ—  ‘mā kho tvaṃ, mārisa, sabbaso āhārupacchedāya paṭipajji.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨂①(devatā) 🔽(tvā)(upasaṅkamati) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(vadati) ⏏️(mā) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(mārisa) ⏏️(sabbaso) 🚹⨀④🆎(āhāra+upaccheda) 🟢⨀🤘⏮(paṭipajjati)  
then | indeed | me | Aggivessana | devas | having approached | this | said | do not | indeed | you | Mārisa | completely | for the cutting off of food | practise  
Then, Aggivessana, devas approached me and said: ‘Do not, Mārisa, practise the complete cutting off of food.

1253.3 Sace kho tvaṃ, mārisa, sabbaso āhārupacchedāya paṭipajjissasi, tassa te mayaṃ dibbaṃ ojaṃ lomakūpehi ajjhohāressāma, tāya tvaṃ yāpessasī’ti.  
⏏️(sace) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(mārisa) ⏏️(sabbaso) 🚹⨀④🆎(āhāra+upaccheda) 🟢⨀🤘⏭(paṭipajjati) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀④(tvaṃ) *️⃣👆⚧⨂①(ahaṃ) 🚺⨀②(dibba) 🚺⨀②(ojā) 🚹⨂③🆎(loma+kūpa) 🟠⨂👆⏭(ajjhohāreti) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🟠⨀🤘⏭(yāpeti) ⏏️(iti)  
if | indeed | you | Mārisa | completely | for the cutting off of food | will practise | to that | to you | we | divine | nectar | into the pores | will infuse | on that | you | will subsist | (end quote)  
If you, Mārisa, practise the complete cutting off of food, we will infuse divine nectar into your pores, and you will subsist on that.’

1253.4 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘ahañceva kho pana sabbaso ajajjitaṃ paṭijāneyyaṃ, imā ca me devatā dibbaṃ ojaṃ lomakūpehi ajjhohāreyyuṃ, tāya cāhaṃ yāpeyyaṃ, taṃ mamassa musā’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(ce) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(pana) ⏏️(sabbaso) 🚻⨀②(ajajjita) 🔵⨀👆⏯(paṭijānāti) *️⃣🤟🚺⨂①(ima) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚺⨂①(devatā) 🚺⨀②(dibba) 🚺⨀②(ojā) 🚹⨂③🆎(loma+kūpa) 🟠⨂🤟⏯(ajjhohāreti) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🔵⨀👆⏯(yāpeti) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(atthi) ⏏️(musā) ⏏️(iti)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | occurred | I | if | just | indeed | moreover | completely | fasting | I should claim | these | and | my | devas | divine | nectar | into the pores | would infuse | on that | and | I | I would subsist | that | on my part | would be | a falsehood | (end quote)  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘If I claim to be completely fasting while these devas infuse divine nectar into my pores and I subsist on that, that would be a falsehood on my part.’

1253.5 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, tā devatā paccācikkhāmi, ‘halan’ti vadāmi.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚺⨂②(ta) 🚺⨂②(devatā) 🟢⨀👆▶️(paccācikkhati) ⏏️(halaṃ) ⏏️(iti) 🟢⨀👆▶️(vadati)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | those | devas | I dismiss | enough | (end quote) | I say  
So, Aggivessana, I dismissed those devas, saying ‘Enough.’

1254.1 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘yannūnāhaṃ thokaṃ thokaṃ āhāraṃ āhāreyyaṃ, pasataṃ pasataṃ, yadi vā muggayūsaṃ, yadi vā kulatthayūsaṃ, yadi vā kaḷāyayūsaṃ, yadi vā hareṇukayūsan’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ya) ⏏️(nūna) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀②(thoka) 🚹⨀②(thoka) 🚹⨀②(āhāra) 🔵⨀👆⏯(āhāreti) 🚹⨀②(pasata) 🚹⨀②(pasata) ⏏️(yadi) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀②🆎(mugga+yūsa) ⏏️(yadi) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀②🆎(kulattha+yūsa) ⏏️(yadi) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀②🆎(kaḷāya+yūsa) ⏏️(yadi) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀②🆎(hareṇuka+yūsa) ⏏️(iti)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | occurred | what | if | I | little | by little | food | I should take | a handful | at a time | whether | or | mung bean soup | whether | or | horse gram soup | whether | or | chickpea soup | whether | or | pea soup | (end quote)  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘What if I were to take very little food, a handful at a time, whether of mung bean soup, or horse gram soup, or chickpea soup, or pea soup?’

1254.2 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, thokaṃ thokaṃ āhāraṃ āhāresiṃ, pasataṃ pasataṃ, yadi vā muggayūsaṃ, yadi vā kulatthayūsaṃ, yadi vā kaḷāyayūsaṃ, yadi vā hareṇukayūsaṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀②(thoka) 🚹⨀②(thoka) 🚹⨀②(āhāra) 🟠⨀👆⏮(āhāreti) 🚹⨀②(pasata) 🚹⨀②(pasata) ⏏️(yadi) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀②🆎(mugga+yūsa) ⏏️(yadi) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀②🆎(kulattha+yūsa) ⏏️(yadi) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀②🆎(kaḷāya+yūsa) ⏏️(yadi) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀②🆎(hareṇuka+yūsa)

So, Aggivessana, I took very little food, a handful at a time, whether of mung bean soup, or horse gram soup, or chickpea soup, or pea soup.

1254.3 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, thokaṃ thokaṃ āhāraṃ āhārayato, pasataṃ pasataṃ, yadi vā muggayūsaṃ, yadi vā kulatthayūsaṃ, yadi vā kaḷāyayūsaṃ, yadi vā hareṇukayūsaṃ, adhimattakasimānaṃ patto kāyo hoti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀②(thoka) 🚹⨀②(thoka) 🚹⨀②(āhāra) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(anta)(āhārayanta) 🚹⨀②(pasata) 🚹⨀②(pasata) ⏏️(yadi) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀②🆎(mugga+yūsa) ⏏️(yadi) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀②🆎(kulattha+yūsa) ⏏️(yadi) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀②🆎(kaḷāya+yūsa) ⏏️(yadi) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨀②🆎(hareṇuka+yūsa) 🚹⨀②🆎(adhimatta+kasima) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(patta) 🚹⨀①(kāya) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti)  
of that | of me | Aggivessana | little | by little | food | taking | a handful | at a time | whether | or | mung bean soup | whether | or | horse gram soup | whether | or | chickpea soup | whether | or | pea soup | a state of extreme emaciation | reached | body | is  
While I was taking very little food, a handful at a time, whether of mung bean soup, or horse gram soup, or chickpea soup, or pea soup, my body reached a state of extreme emaciation.

1254.4 Seyyathāpi nāma āsītikapabbāni vā kāḷapabbāni vā;  
⏏️(seyyathāpi) ⏏️(nāma) 🚻⨂①🆎(āsītika+pabba) ⏏️(vā) 🚻⨂①🆎(kāḷa+pabba) ⏏️(vā)  
just as | indeed | the joints of a liana | or | the joints of the necklace plant | or

⏏️(evaṃ) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(ssu) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚻⨂①🆎(aṅga+paccaṅga) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(bhavati) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀③🔽(tā)🆎(appa+āhāra)  
in the same way | just | indeed | my | limbs | become | because of that | very | eating so little  
Because of eating so little, my limbs became like the joints of a liana or of the necklace plant.

1254.6 Seyyathāpi nāma oṭṭhapadaṃ;  
⏏️(seyyathāpi) ⏏️(nāma) 🚻⨀①🆎(oṭṭha+pada)  
just as | indeed | a camel’s hoof

in the same way | just | indeed | my | backside | is | because of that | very | eating so little  
Because of eating so little, my backside became like a camel’s hoof.

⏏️(evaṃ) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(ssu) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀①🆎(piṭṭhi+kaṇṭaka) 🚹⨀①🆎(uṇṇata+avanata) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀③🔽(tā)🆎(appa+āhāra)  
in the same way | just | indeed | my | spine | raised and lowered | is | because of that | very | eating so little  
Because of eating so little, my spine became raised and lowered like a string of beads.

1254.10 Seyyathāpi nāma jarasālāya gopānasiyo oluggaviluggā bhavanti;  
⏏️(seyyathāpi) ⏏️(nāma) 🚺⨀⑥🆎(jara+sālā) 🚺⨂①(gopānasī) 🚺⨂①🆎(olugga+vilugga) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(bhavati)  
just as | indeed | of an old shed | the rafters | collapsed and hung askew | are

⏏️(evaṃ) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(ssu) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚺⨂①(phāsuḷī) 🚺⨂①🆎(olugga+vilugga) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(bhavati) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀③🔽(tā)🆎(appa+āhāra)  
in the same way | just | indeed | my | ribs | collapsed and hung askew | are | because of that | very | eating so little  
Because of eating so little, my ribs collapsed and hung askew like the rafters of an old shed.

1254.12 Seyyathāpi nāma gambhīre udapāne udakatārakā gambhīragatā okkhāyikā dissanti;

just as | indeed | in a deep | well | the sparkles of the water | sunk deep | receded | are seen

⏏️(evaṃ) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(ssu) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⑦🆎(akkhi+kūpa) 🚺⨂①🆎(akkhi+tārakā) 🚺⨂①🆎(gambhīra+gata) 🚺⨂①(okkhāyika) 🔴⨂🤟▶️(dissati) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀③🔽(tā)🆎(appa+āhāra)  
in the same way | just | indeed | my | in the eye sockets | the pupils of the eyes | sunk deep | receded | are seen | because of that | very | eating so little  
Because of eating so little, the pupils of my eyes appeared deep sunk in their sockets, like the sparkle of the water seen sunk deep in a deep well.

1254.14 Seyyathāpi nāma tittakālābu āmakacchinno vātātapena samphuṭito hoti sammilāto;  
⏏️(seyyathāpi) ⏏️(nāma) 🚹⨀①🆎(tittaka+alābu) 🚹⨀①🆎(āmaka+chinna) 🚹⨀③🆎(vāta+ātapa) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(samphuṭita) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(sammilāta)  
just as | indeed | a bitter gourd | cut while raw | by the wind and sun | shrivelled | is | withered  
1254.15 evamevassu me sīsacchavi samphuṭitā hoti sammilātā tāyevappāhāratāya.  
⏏️(evaṃ) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(ssu) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚺⨀①🆎(sīsa+chavi) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(samphuṭita) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(sammilāta) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀③🔽(tā)🆎(appa+āhāra)  
in the same way | just | indeed | my | scalp | shrivelled | is | withered | because of that | very | eating so little  
Because of eating so little, my scalp became shrivelled and withered like a bitter gourd cut while raw and withered by the wind and sun.

1255.1 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, udaracchaviṃ parimasissāmīti piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṃyeva pariggaṇhāmi, piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṃ parimasissāmīti udaracchaviṃyeva pariggaṇhāmi, yāvassu me, aggivessana, udaracchavi piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṃ allīnā hoti tāyevappāhāratāya.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨀②🆎(udara+chavi) 🟢⨀👆⏭(parimasati) ⏏️(iti) 🚹⨀②🆎(piṭṭhi+kaṇṭaka) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀👆▶️(pariggaṇhāti) 🚹⨀②🆎(piṭṭhi+kaṇṭaka) 🟢⨀👆⏭(parimasati) ⏏️(iti) 🚺⨀②🆎(udara+chavi) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀👆▶️(pariggaṇhāti) ⏏️(yāva) ⏏️(ssu) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨀①🆎(udara+chavi) 🚹⨀②🆎(piṭṭhi+kaṇṭaka) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(allīna) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀③🔽(tā)🆎(appa+āhāra)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | the skin of the belly | I will touch | (end quote) | the spine | just | I grab | the spine | I will touch | (end quote) | the skin of the belly | just | I grab | to such an extent | indeed | my | Aggivessana | the skin of the belly | to the spine | adhered | is | because of that | very | eating so little  
If I thought ‘I will touch the skin of my belly,’ Aggivessana, I grabbed my spine. If I thought ‘I will touch my spine,’ I grabbed the skin of my belly. To such an extent, Aggivessana, did the skin of my belly adhere to my spine because of eating so little.

1255.2 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, vaccaṃ vā muttaṃ vā karissāmīti tattheva avakujjo papatāmi tāyevappāhāratāya.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚻⨀②(vacca) ⏏️(vā) 🚻⨀②(mutta) ⏏️(vā) 🟢⨀👆⏭(karoti) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️(tattha) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀①(avakujja) 🟢⨀👆▶️(papatati) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀③🔽(tā)🆎(appa+āhāra)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | excrement | or | urine | or | I will do | (end quote) | there | right | face down | I fall | because of that | very | eating so little  
If I thought ‘I will defecate or urinate,’ Aggivessana, I fell over on my face right there, because of eating so little.

1255.3 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, imameva kāyaṃ assāsento pāṇinā gattāni anumajjāmi.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀②(kāya) 🚹⨀①🔽(anta)(assāsenta) 🚹⨀③(pāṇi) 🚻⨂②(gatta) 🟢⨀👆▶️(anumajjati)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | this | very | body | easing | with the hand | limbs | I rub  
If I tried to ease this body by rubbing my limbs with my hand, Aggivessana,

1255.4 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, pāṇinā gattāni anumajjato pūtimūlāni lomāni kāyasmā papatanti tāyevappāhāratāya.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀③(pāṇi) 🚻⨂②(gatta) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(anta)(anumajjanta) 🚻⨂①🆎(pūti+mūla) 🚻⨂①(loma) 🚹⨀⑤(kāya) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(papatati) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀③🔽(tā)🆎(appa+āhāra)  
of that | of me | Aggivessana | with the hand | limbs | rubbing | rotted at the roots | hairs | from the body | fall | because of that | very | eating so little  
the hairs, rotted at their roots, fell from my body as I rubbed my limbs with my hand, because of eating so little.

1255.5 Apissu maṃ, aggivessana, manussā disvā evamāhaṃsu—  ‘kāḷo samaṇo gotamo’ti.  
⏏️(api) ⏏️(ssu) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨂①(manussa) 🔽(tvā)(passati) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🟢⨂🤟🔄(āha) 🚹⨀①(kāḷa) 🚹⨀①(samaṇa) 🚹⨀①(gotama) ⏏️(iti)  
and | then | me | Aggivessana | people | having seen | thus | said | black | the wanderer | Gotama | (end quote)  
Then, Aggivessana, people saw me and said: ‘The wanderer Gotama is black.’

1255.6 Ekacce manussā evamāhaṃsu—  ‘na kāḷo samaṇo gotamo, sāmo samaṇo gotamo’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ekacca) 🚹⨂①(manussa) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🟢⨂🤟🔄(āha) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀①(kāḷa) 🚹⨀①(samaṇa) 🚹⨀①(gotama) 🚹⨀①(sāma) 🚹⨀①(samaṇa) 🚹⨀①(gotama) ⏏️(iti)  
some | people | thus | said | not | black | the wanderer | Gotama | brown | the wanderer | Gotama | (end quote)  
Some people said: ‘The wanderer Gotama is not black, the wanderer Gotama is brown.’

1255.7 Ekacce manussā evamāhaṃsu—  ‘na kāḷo samaṇo gotamo, napi sāmo, maṅguracchavi samaṇo gotamo’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ekacca) 🚹⨂①(manussa) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🟢⨂🤟🔄(āha) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀①(kāḷa) 🚹⨀①(samaṇa) 🚹⨀①(gotama) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(api) 🚹⨀①(sāma) 🚹⨀①🆎(maṅgura+chavi) 🚹⨀①(samaṇa) 🚹⨀①(gotama) ⏏️(iti)  
some | people | thus | said | not | black | the wanderer | Gotama | nor | even | brown | with a sallow complexion | the wanderer | Gotama | (end quote)  
Some people said: ‘The wanderer Gotama is not black, nor brown, the wanderer Gotama has a sallow complexion.’

1255.8 Yāvassu me, aggivessana, tāva parisuddho chavivaṇṇo pariyodāto upahato hoti tāyevappāhāratāya.  
⏏️(yāva) ⏏️(ssu) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) ⏏️(tāva) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(parisuddha) 🚹⨀①🆎(chavi+vaṇṇa) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(pariyodāta) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(upahata) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀③🔽(tā)🆎(appa+āhāra)  
to such an extent | indeed | my | Aggivessana | to that extent | pure | complexion | bright | ruined | is | because of that | very | eating so little  
To such an extent, Aggivessana, was my pure and bright complexion ruined because of eating so little.

</Tabs>

## `Jhānā`

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
1256\. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘Whatever wanderers or brahmins in the past experienced painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, this is the utmost, there is nothing beyond this. Whatever wanderers or brahmins in the future will experience painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, this is the utmost, there is nothing beyond this. Whatever wanderers or brahmins at present experience painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, this is the utmost, there is nothing beyond this. Yet by this bitter, difficult austerity, I have not attained any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated. Could there indeed be another path to awakening?’

Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘I recall that when my father the Sakyan was working, while I was sitting in the cool shade of a rose-apple tree, secluding myself from sensual pleasures, detaching from unwholesome characteristics, I entered and remained in the first mental disposition, accompanied by reflection and consideration, secluded from defilements, with felicity and satisfaction. Could this indeed be the path to awakening?’

Then, Aggivessana, there was in me the consciousness following that memory:

> ‘This indeed is the path to awakening.’

Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘Why indeed am I afraid of that pleasure, for it is pleasure apart from sensual pleasures, apart from unwholesome characteristics?’

Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘I am indeed not afraid of that pleasure, for it is pleasure apart from sensual pleasures, apart from unwholesome characteristics.’

1257\. Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me:

> ‘It is not easy to attain that pleasure with a body so extremely emaciated. What if I were to consume solid food, rice and porridge?’

So, Aggivessana, I consumed solid food, rice and porridge. Now at that time, Aggivessana, five bhikkhū were attending on me, thinking:

> ‘Whatever Dhamma the wanderer Gotama realises, he will tell us.’

But when I consumed solid food, rice and porridge, then those five bhikkhū, giving up on me, departed, saying:

> ‘The wanderer Gotama has become luxurious, he has given up striving and reverted to luxury.’

1258\. So, Aggivessana, having consumed solid food and gained strength, secluding myself from sensual pleasures, detaching from unwholesome characteristics, I entered and remained in the first mental disposition, accompanied by reflection and consideration, secluded from defilements, with felicity and satisfaction. Even such pleasant feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain. With the conclusion of reflection and consideration, I entered and remained in the second mental disposition, accompanied by an inner calming of intention, focused, without reflection, without consideration, born from focus, with felicity and satisfaction. Even such pleasant feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain. With the detachment from felicity, I remained equanimous, aware and clearly comprehending, and experienced body comfort, as the liberated ones describe: ‘equanimous, aware, living at ease’ — I entered and remained in the third mental disposition. Even such pleasant feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain. With the giving up of the pleasant and the unpleasant, and with the previous disappearance of mental pleasure and displeasure, I entered and remained in the fourth mental disposition, in a neutral state, and possessing purification of awareness by equanimity. Even such pleasant feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
1256\. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘ye kho keci atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā opakkamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayiṃsu, etāvaparamaṃ, nayito bhiyyo. Yepi hi keci anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā opakkamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayissanti, etāvaparamaṃ, nayito bhiyyo. Yepi hi keci etarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā opakkamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayanti, etāvaparamaṃ, nayito bhiyyo. Na kho panāhaṃ imāya kaṭukāya dukkarakārikāya adhigacchāmi uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesaṃ. Siyā nu kho añño maggo bodhāyā’ti? Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘abhijānāmi kho panāhaṃ pitu sakkassa kammante sītāya jambucchāyāya nisinno vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharitā. Siyā nu kho eso maggo bodhāyā’ti? Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, satānusāri viññāṇaṃ ahosi—  ‘eseva maggo bodhāyā’ti. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘kiṃ nu kho ahaṃ tassa sukhassa bhāyāmi, yaṃ taṃ sukhaṃ aññatreva kāmehi aññatra akusalehi dhammehī’ti? Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘na kho ahaṃ tassa sukhassa bhāyāmi, yaṃ taṃ sukhaṃ aññatreva kāmehi aññatra akusalehi dhammehī’ti.

1257\. Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘na kho taṃ sukaraṃ sukhaṃ adhigantuṃ evaṃ adhimattakasimānaṃ pattakāyena, yannūnāhaṃ oḷārikaṃ āhāraṃ āhāreyyaṃ odanakummāsan’ti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, oḷārikaṃ āhāraṃ āhāresiṃ odanakummāsaṃ. Tena kho pana maṃ, aggivessana, samayena pañca bhikkhū paccupaṭṭhitā honti—  ‘yaṃ kho samaṇo gotamo dhammaṃ adhigamissati, taṃ no ārocessatī’ti. Yato kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, oḷārikaṃ āhāraṃ āhāresiṃ odanakummāsaṃ, atha me te pañca bhikkhū nibbijja pakkamiṃsu—  ‘bāhulliko samaṇo gotamo, padhānavibbhanto, āvatto bāhullāyā’ti.

1258\. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, oḷārikaṃ āhāraṃ āhāretvā, balaṃ gahetvā, vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihāsiṃ. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā sukhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati. Vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvaṃ avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihāsiṃ. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā sukhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati. Pītiyā ca virāgā upekkhako ca vihāsiṃ, sato ca sampajāno. Sukhañca kāyena paṭisaṃvedesiṃ yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti—  ‘upekkhako satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihāsiṃ. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā sukhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati. Sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbeva somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ upekkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihāsiṃ. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā sukhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
1256\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀬𑁂 𑀔𑁄 𑀓𑁂𑀘𑀺 𑀅𑀢𑀻𑀢𑀫𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀯𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀯𑀸 𑀑𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀢𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸 𑀔𑀭𑀸 𑀓𑀝𑀼𑀓𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀬𑀺𑀁𑀲𑀼, 𑀏𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀧𑀭𑀫𑀁, 𑀦𑀬𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀪𑀺𑀬𑁆𑀬𑁄. 𑀬𑁂𑀧𑀺 𑀳𑀺 𑀓𑁂𑀘𑀺 𑀅𑀦𑀸𑀕𑀢𑀫𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀯𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀯𑀸 𑀑𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀢𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸 𑀔𑀭𑀸 𑀓𑀝𑀼𑀓𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀬𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀏𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀧𑀭𑀫𑀁, 𑀦𑀬𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀪𑀺𑀬𑁆𑀬𑁄. 𑀬𑁂𑀧𑀺 𑀳𑀺 𑀓𑁂𑀘𑀺 𑀏𑀢𑀭𑀳𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀯𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀯𑀸 𑀑𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸 𑀢𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸 𑀔𑀭𑀸 𑀓𑀝𑀼𑀓𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀏𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀧𑀭𑀫𑀁, 𑀦𑀬𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀪𑀺𑀬𑁆𑀬𑁄. 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀇𑀫𑀸𑀬 𑀓𑀝𑀼𑀓𑀸𑀬 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀭𑀓𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀺 𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀮𑀫𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀁. 𑀲𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀦𑀼 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑁄 𑀫𑀕𑁆𑀕𑁄 𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺? 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀧𑀺𑀢𑀼 𑀲𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀓𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀲𑀻𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀚𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸𑀬𑀸𑀬 𑀦𑀺𑀲𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀦𑁄 𑀯𑀺𑀯𑀺𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀯𑀺𑀯𑀺𑀘𑁆𑀘 𑀅𑀓𑀼𑀲𑀮𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀲𑀯𑀺𑀢𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀁 𑀲𑀯𑀺𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀓𑀚𑀁 𑀧𑀻𑀢𑀺𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀁 𑀧𑀞𑀫𑀁 𑀛𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀺𑀢𑀸. 𑀲𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀦𑀼 𑀔𑁄 𑀏𑀲𑁄 𑀫𑀕𑁆𑀕𑁄 𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺? 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀲𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀼𑀲𑀸𑀭𑀺 𑀯𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀁 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀏𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀫𑀕𑁆𑀕𑁄 𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀓𑀺𑀁 𑀦𑀼 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀪𑀸𑀬𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀬𑀁 𑀢𑀁 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀁 𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀢𑁆𑀭𑁂𑀯 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀢𑁆𑀭 𑀅𑀓𑀼𑀲𑀮𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂𑀳𑀻’𑀢𑀺? 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀪𑀸𑀬𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀬𑀁 𑀢𑀁 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀁 𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀢𑁆𑀭𑁂𑀯 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀢𑁆𑀭 𑀅𑀓𑀼𑀲𑀮𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂𑀳𑀻’𑀢𑀺.

1257\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀢𑀁 𑀲𑀼𑀓𑀭𑀁 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀁 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀼𑀁 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀓𑀲𑀺𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀓𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀦, 𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀑𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁 𑀑𑀤𑀦𑀓𑀼𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀑𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑁂𑀲𑀺𑀁 𑀑𑀤𑀦𑀓𑀼𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀁. 𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀲𑀫𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀼𑀧𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀢𑀸 𑀳𑁄𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺—  ‘𑀬𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀫𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀢𑀁 𑀦𑁄 𑀆𑀭𑁄𑀘𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺. 𑀬𑀢𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀑𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑁂𑀲𑀺𑀁 𑀑𑀤𑀦𑀓𑀼𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀁, 𑀅𑀣 𑀫𑁂 𑀢𑁂 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁𑀲𑀼—  ‘𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀺𑀓𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄, 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀸𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺.

1258\. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀑𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀁 𑀆𑀳𑀸𑀭𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸, 𑀩𑀮𑀁 𑀕𑀳𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸, 𑀯𑀺𑀯𑀺𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀯𑀺𑀯𑀺𑀘𑁆𑀘 𑀅𑀓𑀼𑀲𑀮𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀲𑀯𑀺𑀢𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀁 𑀲𑀯𑀺𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀓𑀚𑀁 𑀧𑀻𑀢𑀺𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀁 𑀧𑀞𑀫𑀁 𑀛𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺. 𑀯𑀺𑀢𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀯𑀺𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀯𑀽𑀧𑀲𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀲𑀸𑀤𑀦𑀁 𑀘𑁂𑀢𑀲𑁄 𑀏𑀓𑁄𑀤𑀺𑀪𑀸𑀯𑀁 𑀅𑀯𑀺𑀢𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀁 𑀅𑀯𑀺𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀚𑀁 𑀧𑀻𑀢𑀺𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀁 𑀤𑀼𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀛𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺. 𑀧𑀻𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀘 𑀯𑀺𑀭𑀸𑀕𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑁂𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀓𑁄 𑀘 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁, 𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀘 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑀸𑀦𑁄. 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀓𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀲𑀁𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀺𑀁 𑀬𑀁 𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀆𑀘𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺—  ‘𑀉𑀧𑁂𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀓𑁄 𑀲𑀢𑀺𑀫𑀸 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀻’𑀢𑀺 𑀢𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀛𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀘 𑀧𑀳𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀘 𑀧𑀳𑀸𑀦𑀸, 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂𑀯 𑀲𑁄𑀫𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀤𑁄𑀫𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀗𑁆𑀕𑀫𑀸, 𑀅𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀫𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑁂𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀸𑀲𑀢𑀺𑀧𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀺𑀁 𑀘𑀢𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀁 𑀛𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺.

1256.1 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘ye kho keci atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā opakkamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayiṃsu, etāvaparamaṃ, nayito bhiyyo.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ya) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(kaci) 🚹⨀②🔽(ta)(atīta) 🚹⨀②(addha) 🚹⨂①(samaṇa) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨂①(brāhmaṇa) ⏏️(vā) 🚺⨂①(opakkamika) 🚺⨂①(dukkha) 🚺⨂①🔽(ta)(tibba) 🚺⨂①(khara) 🚺⨂①(kaṭuka) 🚺⨂①(vedanā) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(vedayati) 🚻⨀①🆎(etāvant+parama) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(ito) ⏏️(bhiyyo)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | occurred | whatever | indeed | any | past | time | wanderers | or | brahmins | or | due to exertion | painful | sharp | harsh | bitter | feelings | experienced | this is the utmost | not | than this | more  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘Whatever wanderers or brahmins in the past experienced painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, this is the utmost, there is nothing beyond this.

1256.2 Yepi hi keci anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā opakkamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayissanti, etāvaparamaṃ, nayito bhiyyo.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ya) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(hi) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(kaci) 🚹⨀②🔽(ta)🆎(na+āgata) 🚹⨀②(addha) 🚹⨂①(samaṇa) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨂①(brāhmaṇa) ⏏️(vā) 🚺⨂①(opakkamika) 🚺⨂①(dukkha) 🚺⨂①🔽(ta)(tibba) 🚺⨂①(khara) 🚺⨂①(kaṭuka) 🚺⨂①(vedanā) 🟢⨂🤟⏭(vedayati) 🚻⨀①🆎(etāvant+parama) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(ito) ⏏️(bhiyyo)  
whatever | too | indeed | any | future | time | wanderers | or | brahmins | or | due to exertion | painful | sharp | harsh | bitter | feelings | will experience | this is the utmost | not | than this | more  
Whatever wanderers or brahmins in the future will experience painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, this is the utmost, there is nothing beyond this.

1256.3 Yepi hi keci etarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā opakkamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vedanā vedayanti, etāvaparamaṃ, nayito bhiyyo.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ya) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(hi) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(kaci) ⏏️(etarahi) 🚹⨂①(samaṇa) ⏏️(vā) 🚹⨂①(brāhmaṇa) ⏏️(vā) 🚺⨂①(opakkamika) 🚺⨂①(dukkha) 🚺⨂①🔽(ta)(tibba) 🚺⨂①(khara) 🚺⨂①(kaṭuka) 🚺⨂①(vedanā) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(vedayati) 🚻⨀①🆎(etāvant+parama) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(ito) ⏏️(bhiyyo)  
whatever | too | indeed | any | now | wanderers | or | brahmins | or | due to exertion | painful | sharp | harsh | bitter | feelings | experience | this is the utmost | not | than this | more  
Whatever wanderers or brahmins at present experience painful, sharp, harsh, bitter feelings due to exertion, this is the utmost, there is nothing beyond this.

1256.4 Na kho panāhaṃ imāya kaṭukāya dukkarakārikāya adhigacchāmi uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesaṃ.  
⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(pana) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ima) 🚺⨀③(kaṭuka) 🚺⨀③🆎(dukkara+kārikā) 🟢⨀👆▶️(adhigacchati) ⏏️(uttari) 🚹⨀⑤🆎(manussa+dhamma) 🚹⨀②🆎(alaṃ+ariya+ñāṇa+dassana+visesa)  
not | indeed | but | I | by this | bitter | difficult asceticism | I attain | beyond | the human state | distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated  
Yet by this bitter, difficult austerity, I have not attained any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated.

1256.5 Siyā nu kho añño maggo bodhāyā’ti?  
🟢⨀🤟⏯(atthi) ⏏️(nu) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(añña) 🚹⨀①(magga) 🚹⨀④(bodha) ⏏️(iti)  
could be | (question) | indeed | another | path | to awakening | (end quote)  
Could there indeed be another path to awakening?’

1256.6 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘abhijānāmi kho panāhaṃ pitu sakkassa kammante sītāya jambucchāyāya nisinno vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharitā.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🟢⨀👆▶️(abhijānāti) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(pana) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⑥(pitar) 🚹⨀⑥(sakka) 🚹⨀⑦(kammanta) 🚺⨀⑦(sīta) 🚺⨀⑦🆎(jambu+chāyā) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(nisinna) 🔽(ya)(viviccati) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨂⑤(kāma) 🔽(ya)(viviccati) 🚹⨂⑤🆎(na+kusala) 🚹⨂⑤(dhamma) 🚻⨀②🆎(sa+vitakka) 🚻⨀②🆎(sa+vicāra) 🚻⨀②🆎(viveka+ja) 🚻⨀②🆎(pīti+sukha) 🚻⨀②(paṭhama) 🚻⨀②(jhāna) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🚹⨀①🔽(tu)(viharitar)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | occurred | I recall | indeed | but | I | of my father | of the Sakyan | during the work | in the cool | in the shade of a rose-apple tree | seated | secluding oneself from | just | sensual pleasures | detaching from | unwholesome | characteristics | accompanied by reflection | with consideration | secluded from defilements | with felicity and satisfaction | first | mental disposition | entering | one who remained in  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘I recall that when my father the Sakyan was working, while I was sitting in the cool shade of a rose-apple tree, secluding myself from sensual pleasures, detaching from unwholesome characteristics, I entered and remained in the first mental disposition, accompanied by reflection and consideration, secluded from defilements, with felicity and satisfaction.

1256.7 Siyā nu kho eso maggo bodhāyā’ti?  
🟢⨀🤟⏯(atthi) ⏏️(nu) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(eta) 🚹⨀①(magga) 🚹⨀④(bodha) ⏏️(iti)  
could be | (question) | indeed | this | path | to awakening | (end quote)  
Could this indeed be the path to awakening?’

1256.8 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, satānusāri viññāṇaṃ ahosi—  ‘eseva maggo bodhāyā’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚻⨀①🆎(sati+anusārī) 🚻⨀①(viññāṇa) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(eta) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀①(magga) 🚹⨀④(bodha) ⏏️(iti)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | following the memory | consciousness | there was | this | indeed | path | to awakening | (end quote)  
Then, Aggivessana, there was in me the consciousness following that memory: ‘This indeed is the path to awakening.’

1256.9 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘kiṃ nu kho ahaṃ tassa sukhassa bhāyāmi, yaṃ taṃ sukhaṃ aññatreva kāmehi aññatra akusalehi dhammehī’ti?  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(kiṃ) ⏏️(nu) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀⑥(ta) 🚻⨀⑥(sukha) 🟢⨀👆▶️(bhāyati) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ya) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ta) 🚻⨀①(sukha) ⏏️(aññatra) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨂⑤(kāma) ⏏️(aññatra) 🚹⨂⑤🆎(na+kusala) 🚹⨂⑤(dhamma) ⏏️(iti)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | occurred | why | (question) | indeed | I | of that | of pleasure | I am afraid | which | that | pleasure | apart from | just | sensual pleasures | apart from | unwholesome | characteristics | (end quote)  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘Why indeed am I afraid of that pleasure, for it is pleasure apart from sensual pleasures, apart from unwholesome characteristics?’

1256.10 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘na kho ahaṃ tassa sukhassa bhāyāmi, yaṃ taṃ sukhaṃ aññatreva kāmehi aññatra akusalehi dhammehī’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀⑥(ta) 🚻⨀⑥(sukha) 🟢⨀👆▶️(bhāyati) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ya) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ta) 🚻⨀①(sukha) ⏏️(aññatra) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨂⑤(kāma) ⏏️(aññatra) 🚹⨂⑤🆎(na+kusala) 🚹⨂⑤(dhamma) ⏏️(iti)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | occurred | not | indeed | I | of that | of pleasure | I am afraid | which | that | pleasure | apart from | just | sensual pleasures | apart from | unwholesome | characteristics | (end quote)  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘I am indeed not afraid of that pleasure, for it is pleasure apart from sensual pleasures, apart from unwholesome characteristics.’

1257.1 Tassa mayhaṃ, aggivessana, etadahosi—  ‘na kho taṃ sukaraṃ sukhaṃ adhigantuṃ evaṃ adhimattakasimānaṃ pattakāyena, yannūnāhaṃ oḷārikaṃ āhāraṃ āhāreyyaṃ odanakummāsan’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ta) 🚻⨀①🆎(su+kara) 🚻⨀①(sukha) 🔽(tuṃ)(adhigacchati) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🚹⨀②🆎(adhimatta+kasima) 🚹⨀③🆎(patta+kāya) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ya) ⏏️(nūna) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀②(oḷārika) 🚹⨀②(āhāra) 🔵⨀👆⏯(āhāreti) 🚹⨀②🆎(odana+kummāsa) ⏏️(iti)  
to that | to me | Aggivessana | this | occurred | not | indeed | that | easy to do | pleasure | to attain | so | extreme emaciation | with a body that has reached | what | if | I | solid | food | I should consume | rice and porridge | (end quote)  
Then, Aggivessana, this thought occurred to me: ‘It is not easy to attain that pleasure with a body so extremely emaciated. What if I were to consume solid food, rice and porridge?’

1257.2 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, oḷārikaṃ āhāraṃ āhāresiṃ odanakummāsaṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀②(oḷārika) 🚹⨀②(āhāra) 🟠⨀👆⏮(āhāreti) 🚹⨀②🆎(odana+kummāsa)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | solid | food | I consumed | rice and porridge  
So, Aggivessana, I consumed solid food, rice and porridge.

1257.3 Tena kho pana maṃ, aggivessana, samayena pañca bhikkhū paccupaṭṭhitā honti—  ‘yaṃ kho samaṇo gotamo dhammaṃ adhigamissati, taṃ no ārocessatī’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(pana) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀③(samaya) ⚧⨂①(pañca) 🚹⨂①(bhikkhu) 🚹⨂①🔽(ta)(paccupaṭṭhita) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(hoti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(samaṇa) 🚹⨀①(gotama) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟢⨀🤟⏭(adhigacchati) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨂④(ahaṃ) 🟠⨀🤟⏭(āroceti) ⏏️(iti)  
at that | indeed | now | me | Aggivessana | time | five | bhikkhū | attending on | are | whatever | indeed | the wanderer | Gotama | Dhamma | will realise | that | to us | will tell | (end quote)  
Now at that time, Aggivessana, five bhikkhū were attending on me, thinking: ‘Whatever Dhamma the wanderer Gotama realises, he will tell us.’

1257.4 Yato kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, oḷārikaṃ āhāraṃ āhāresiṃ odanakummāsaṃ, atha me te pañca bhikkhū nibbijja pakkamiṃsu—  ‘bāhulliko samaṇo gotamo, padhānavibbhanto, āvatto bāhullāyā’ti.  
⏏️(yato) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀②(oḷārika) 🚹⨀②(āhāra) 🟠⨀👆⏮(āhāreti) 🚹⨀②🆎(odana+kummāsa) ⏏️(atha) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ta) ⚧⨂①(pañca) 🚹⨂①(bhikkhu) 🔽(ya)(nibbindati) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(pakkamati) 🚹⨀①(bāhullika) 🚹⨀①(samaṇa) 🚹⨀①(gotama) 🚹⨀①🆎(padhāna+vibbhanta) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(āvatta) 🚻⨀④(bāhulla) ⏏️(iti)  
when | indeed | I | Aggivessana | solid | food | I consumed | rice and porridge | then | me | those | five | bhikkhū | having given up | departed | luxurious | the wanderer | Gotama | given up striving | reverted | to luxury | (end quote)  
But when I consumed solid food, rice and porridge, then those five bhikkhū, giving up on me, departed, saying: ‘The wanderer Gotama has become luxurious, he has given up striving and reverted to luxury.’

1258.1 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, oḷārikaṃ āhāraṃ āhāretvā, balaṃ gahetvā, vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihāsiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀②(oḷārika) 🚹⨀②(āhāra) 🔽(tvā)(āhāreti) 🚻⨀②(bala) 🔽(tvā)(gaṇhāti) 🔽(ya)(viviccati) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨂⑤(kāma) 🔽(ya)(viviccati) 🚹⨂⑤🆎(na+kusala) 🚹⨂⑤(dhamma) 🚻⨀②🆎(sa+vitakka) 🚻⨀②🆎(sa+vicāra) 🚻⨀②🆎(viveka+ja) 🚻⨀②🆎(pīti+sukha) 🚻⨀②(paṭhama) 🚻⨀②(jhāna) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆⏮(viharati)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | solid | food | having consumed | strength | having gained | secluding oneself from | just | sensual pleasures | detaching from | unwholesome | characteristics | accompanied by reflection | with consideration | secluded from defilements | with felicity and satisfaction | first | mental disposition | entering | I remained in  
So, Aggivessana, having consumed solid food and gained strength, secluding myself from sensual pleasures, detaching from unwholesome characteristics, I entered and remained in the first mental disposition, accompanied by reflection and consideration, secluded from defilements, with felicity and satisfaction.

1258.2 Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā sukhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.  
🚺⨀①🆎(evaṃ+rūpa) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(uppanna) 🚺⨀①(sukha) 🚺⨀①(vedanā) 🚻⨀②(citta) ⏏️(na) 🔽(ya)(pariyādiyati) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(tiṭṭhati)  
such | even | indeed | my | Aggivessana | arisen | pleasant | feeling | the mind | not | invading | remains  
Even such pleasant feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

1258.3 Vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvaṃ avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihāsiṃ.

of reflection and consideration | with the conclusion | inner | calming | of intention | focused | without reflection | without consideration | born from focus | with felicity and satisfaction | second | mental disposition | entering | I remained in  
With the conclusion of reflection and consideration, I entered and remained in the second mental disposition, accompanied by an inner calming of intention, focused, without reflection, without consideration, born from focus, with felicity and satisfaction.

1258.4 Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā sukhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.  
🚺⨀①🆎(evaṃ+rūpa) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(uppanna) 🚺⨀①(sukha) 🚺⨀①(vedanā) 🚻⨀②(citta) ⏏️(na) 🔽(ya)(pariyādiyati) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(tiṭṭhati)

Even such pleasant feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

With the detachment from felicity, I remained equanimous, aware and clearly comprehending,

and experienced body comfort, as the liberated ones describe: ‘equanimous, aware, living at ease’ — I entered and remained in the third mental disposition.

1258.7 Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā sukhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.

Even such pleasant feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

1258.8 Sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbeva somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ upekkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihāsiṃ.  
🚻⨀⑥(sukha) ⏏️(ca) 🚻⨀⑤(pahāna) 🚻⨀⑥(dukkha) ⏏️(ca) 🚻⨀⑤(pahāna) ⏏️(pubbe) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨂⑥🆎(somanassa+domanassa) 🚹⨀⑤🆎(attha+gama) 🚻⨀②🆎(na+dukkha+na+sukha) 🚻⨀②🆎(upekkhā+sati+pārisuddhi) 🚻⨀②(catuttha) 🚻⨀②(jhāna) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆⏮(viharati)  
of the pleasant | and | with the giving up | of the unpleasant | and | with the giving up | before | just | of mental pleasure and displeasure | with the disappearance | neutral | possessing purification of awareness by equanimity | fourth | mental disposition | entering | I remained in  
With the giving up of the pleasant and the unpleasant, and with the previous disappearance of mental pleasure and displeasure, I entered and remained in the fourth mental disposition, in a neutral state, and possessing purification of awareness by equanimity.

1258.9 Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā sukhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.

## The Three Knowledges

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
1259\. When my mind was thus focused, purified, bright, unblemished, rid of imperfection, malleable, wieldy, steady, and attained to imperturbability, I directed it to knowledge of the recollection of past lives. I recollected my manifold past lives, that is, one birth... thus with their aspects and particulars, I recollected my manifold past lives. This was the first knowledge attained by me, Aggivessana, in the first watch of the night. Ignorance was dispelled, knowledge arose; darkness was dispelled, light arose, as happens in one who dwells diligent, ardent, and resolute. Even such pleasant feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

1260\. When my mind was thus focused, purified, bright, unblemished, rid of imperfection, malleable, wieldy, steady, and attained to imperturbability, I directed it to knowledge of the passing away and reappearance of beings. With the divine eye, which is purified and surpasses the human, I saw beings passing away and reappearing, inferior and superior, fair and ugly, fortunate and unfortunate. I understood how beings pass on according to their actions… This was the second knowledge attained by me, Aggivessana, in the middle watch of the night. Ignorance was dispelled, knowledge arose; darkness was dispelled, light arose, as happens in one who dwells diligent, ardent, and resolute. Even such pleasant feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

1261\. When my mind was thus focused, purified, bright, unblemished, rid of imperfection, malleable, wieldy, steady, and attained to imperturbability, I directed it to knowledge of the destruction of the `āsavā` (defilements). I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is `dukkha` (suffering)’; I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is the origin of suffering’; I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is the cessation of suffering’; I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is the path leading to the cessation of suffering.’ I experientially understood as it really is: ‘These are the defilements’; I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is the origin of the defilements’; I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is the cessation of the defilements’; I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is the path leading to the cessation of the defilements.’ As I knew and saw this, my mind was liberated from the defilement of sensual pleasure, from the defilement of existence, and from the defilement of ignorance. In liberation there was the knowledge: ‘liberated’. I experientially understood: ‘Birth (of suffering) is finished, the pure life is lived, what had to be done is done, there is nothing further for this present state of existence.’ This was the third knowledge attained by me, Aggivessana, in the last watch of the night. Ignorance was dispelled, knowledge arose; darkness was dispelled, light arose, as happens in one who dwells diligent, ardent, and resolute. Even such pleasant feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
1259\. So evaṃ samāhite citte parisuddhe pariyodāte anaṅgaṇe vigatūpakkilese mudubhūte kammaniye ṭhite āneñjappatte pubbenivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhininnāmesiṃ. So anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarāmi, seyyathidaṃ—  ekampi jātiṃ…pe…  iti sākāraṃ sauddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarāmi. Ayaṃ kho me, aggivessana, rattiyā paṭhame yāme paṭhamā vijjā adhigatā; avijjā vihatā, vijjā uppannā; tamo vihato, āloko uppanno; yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpino pahitattassa viharato. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā sukhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.

1260\. So evaṃ samāhite citte parisuddhe pariyodāte anaṅgaṇe vigatūpakkilese mudubhūte kammaniye ṭhite āneñjappatte sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhininnāmesiṃ. So dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena satte passāmi cavamāne upapajjamāne hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate yathākammūpage satte pajānāmi…pe…  ayaṃ kho me, aggivessana, rattiyā majjhime yāme dutiyā vijjā adhigatā; avijjā vihatā, vijjā uppannā; tamo vihato, āloko uppanno; yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpino pahitattassa viharato. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā sukhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.

1261\. So evaṃ samāhite citte parisuddhe pariyodāte anaṅgaṇe vigatūpakkilese mudubhūte kammaniye ṭhite āneñjappatte āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhininnāmesiṃ. So ‘idaṃ dukkhan’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayo’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhanirodho’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ. ‘Ime āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayo’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavanirodho’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavanirodhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ. Tassa me evaṃ jānato evaṃ passato kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuccittha, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuccittha, avijjāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuccittha. Vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ ahosi. ‘Khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhaññāsiṃ. Ayaṃ kho me, aggivessana, rattiyā pacchime yāme tatiyā vijjā adhigatā; avijjā vihatā, vijjā uppannā; tamo vihato, āloko uppanno; yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpino pahitattassa viharato. Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā sukhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
1259\. 𑀲𑁄 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀳𑀺𑀢𑁂 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀤𑀸𑀢𑁂 𑀅𑀦𑀗𑁆𑀕𑀡𑁂 𑀯𑀺𑀕𑀢𑀽𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑁂 𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀼𑀪𑀽𑀢𑁂 𑀓𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀦𑀺𑀬𑁂 𑀞𑀺𑀢𑁂 𑀆𑀦𑁂𑀜𑁆𑀚𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂𑀦𑀺𑀯𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑀺𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀸𑀬 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀲𑀺𑀁. 𑀲𑁄 𑀅𑀦𑁂𑀓𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀺𑀢𑀁 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂𑀦𑀺𑀯𑀸𑀲𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀺𑀤𑀁—  𑀏𑀓𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀺 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀁…𑀧𑁂…  𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀲𑀸𑀓𑀸𑀭𑀁 𑀲𑁅𑀤𑁆𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑁂𑀓𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀺𑀢𑀁 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂𑀦𑀺𑀯𑀸𑀲𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀺. 𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀭𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀞𑀫𑁂 𑀬𑀸𑀫𑁂 𑀧𑀞𑀫𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀸 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀢𑀸; 𑀅𑀯𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀢𑀸, 𑀯𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸; 𑀢𑀫𑁄 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀢𑁄, 𑀆𑀮𑁄𑀓𑁄 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑁄; 𑀬𑀣𑀸 𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀆𑀢𑀸𑀧𑀺𑀦𑁄 𑀧𑀳𑀺𑀢𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀢𑁄. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺.

1260\. 𑀲𑁄 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀳𑀺𑀢𑁂 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀤𑀸𑀢𑁂 𑀅𑀦𑀗𑁆𑀕𑀡𑁂 𑀯𑀺𑀕𑀢𑀽𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑁂 𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀼𑀪𑀽𑀢𑁂 𑀓𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀦𑀺𑀬𑁂 𑀞𑀺𑀢𑁂 𑀆𑀦𑁂𑀜𑁆𑀚𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀘𑀼𑀢𑀽𑀧𑀧𑀸𑀢𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀸𑀬 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀲𑀺𑀁. 𑀲𑁄 𑀤𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂𑀦 𑀘𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀼𑀦𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀲𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀦 𑀅𑀢𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀼𑀲𑀓𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀘𑀯𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁂 𑀉𑀧𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁂 𑀳𑀻𑀦𑁂 𑀧𑀡𑀻𑀢𑁂 𑀲𑀼𑀯𑀡𑁆𑀡𑁂 𑀤𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀡𑁆𑀡𑁂 𑀲𑀼𑀕𑀢𑁂 𑀤𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀢𑁂 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀓𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀽𑀧𑀕𑁂 𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀧𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀺…𑀧𑁂…  𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀭𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀫𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀺𑀫𑁂 𑀬𑀸𑀫𑁂 𑀤𑀼𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀸 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀢𑀸; 𑀅𑀯𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀢𑀸, 𑀯𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸; 𑀢𑀫𑁄 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀢𑁄, 𑀆𑀮𑁄𑀓𑁄 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑁄; 𑀬𑀣𑀸 𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀆𑀢𑀸𑀧𑀺𑀦𑁄 𑀧𑀳𑀺𑀢𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀢𑁄. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺.

1261\. 𑀲𑁄 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀳𑀺𑀢𑁂 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀤𑀸𑀢𑁂 𑀅𑀦𑀗𑁆𑀕𑀡𑁂 𑀯𑀺𑀕𑀢𑀽𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑁂 𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀼𑀪𑀽𑀢𑁂 𑀓𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀦𑀺𑀬𑁂 𑀞𑀺𑀢𑁂 𑀆𑀦𑁂𑀜𑁆𑀚𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀆𑀲𑀯𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀔𑀬𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀸𑀬 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀲𑀺𑀁. 𑀲𑁄 ‘𑀇𑀤𑀁 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀪𑀽𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁, ‘𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀲𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀬𑁄’𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀪𑀽𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁, ‘𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀦𑀺𑀭𑁄𑀥𑁄’𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀪𑀽𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁, ‘𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀦𑀺𑀭𑁄𑀥𑀕𑀸𑀫𑀺𑀦𑀻 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀤𑀸’𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀪𑀽𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁. ‘𑀇𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀲𑀯𑀸’𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀪𑀽𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁, ‘𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀆𑀲𑀯𑀲𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀬𑁄’𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀪𑀽𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁, ‘𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀆𑀲𑀯𑀦𑀺𑀭𑁄𑀥𑁄’𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀪𑀽𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁, ‘𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀆𑀲𑀯𑀦𑀺𑀭𑁄𑀥𑀕𑀸𑀫𑀺𑀦𑀻 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀤𑀸’𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀪𑀽𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑁂 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀢𑁄 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀯𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀣, 𑀪𑀯𑀸𑀲𑀯𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀣, 𑀅𑀯𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀸𑀲𑀯𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀣. 𑀯𑀺𑀫𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀫𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀫𑀺𑀢𑀺 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀁 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺. ‘𑀔𑀻𑀡𑀸 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺, 𑀯𑀼𑀲𑀺𑀢𑀁 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁, 𑀓𑀢𑀁 𑀓𑀭𑀡𑀻𑀬𑀁, 𑀦𑀸𑀧𑀭𑀁 𑀇𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁. 𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀭𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀫𑁂 𑀬𑀸𑀫𑁂 𑀢𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀸 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀢𑀸; 𑀅𑀯𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀢𑀸, 𑀯𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸; 𑀢𑀫𑁄 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀢𑁄, 𑀆𑀮𑁄𑀓𑁄 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑁄; 𑀬𑀣𑀸 𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀆𑀢𑀸𑀧𑀺𑀦𑁄 𑀧𑀳𑀺𑀢𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀢𑁄. 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀼𑀔𑀸 𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀸 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀢𑀺.
</TabItem>
<TabItem label="Interlinear">
1259.1 So evaṃ samāhite citte parisuddhe pariyodāte anaṅgaṇe vigatūpakkilese mudubhūte kammaniye ṭhite āneñjappatte pubbenivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhininnāmesiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(samāhita) 🚻⨀⑦(citta) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(parisuddha) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(pariyodāta) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(na+aṅgaṇa) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(vigata+upakkilesa) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(mudu+bhūta) 🚻⨀⑦(kammaniya) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(ṭhita) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(āneñja+patta) 🚻⨀④🆎(pubbe+nivāsa+anussati+ñāṇa) 🚻⨀②(citta) 🟠⨀👆⏮(abhininnāmeti)  
I | thus | focused | when my mind was | purified | bright | unblemished | rid of imperfection | malleable | wieldy | steady | attained to imperturbability | to knowledge of the recollection of past lives | mind | I directed  
When my mind was thus focused, purified, bright, unblemished, rid of imperfection, malleable, wieldy, steady, and attained to imperturbability, I directed it to knowledge of the recollection of past lives.

1259.2 So anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarāmi, seyyathidaṃ—  ekampi jātiṃ…pe…  iti sākāraṃ sauddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarāmi.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) 🚹⨀②🆎(na+eka+vihita) 🚹⨀②🆎(pubbe+nivāsa) 🟢⨀👆▶️(anussarati) ⏏️(seyyathidaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀②(eka) ⏏️(api) 🚺⨀②(jāti) ⏏️(peyyāla) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️🆎(sa+ākāra) ⏏️🆎(sa+uddesa) 🚹⨀②🆎(na+eka+vihita) 🚹⨀②🆎(pubbe+nivāsa) 🟢⨀👆▶️(anussarati)  
I | manifold | past lives | I recollect | that is | one | too | birth | (and so on) | thus | with their aspects | with their particulars | manifold | past lives | I recollect  
I recollected my manifold past lives, that is, one birth... thus with their aspects and particulars, I recollected my manifold past lives.

1259.3 Ayaṃ kho me, aggivessana, rattiyā paṭhame yāme paṭhamā vijjā adhigatā;  
*️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(ima) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨀⑥(ratti) 🚹⨀⑦(paṭhama) 🚹⨀⑦(yāma) 🚺⨀①(paṭhama) 🚺⨀①(vijjā) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(adhigata)  
this | indeed | by me | Aggivessana | of the night | in the first | watch | first | knowledge | attained  
This was the first knowledge attained by me, Aggivessana, in the first watch of the night.

1259.4 avijjā vihatā, vijjā uppannā;  
🚺⨀①🆎(na+vijjā) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(vihata) 🚺⨀①(vijjā) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(uppanna)  
ignorance | dispelled | knowledge | arisen

🚹⨀①(tamas) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(vihata) 🚹⨀①(āloka) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(uppanna)

darkness was dispelled, light arose,

1259.6 yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpino pahitattassa viharato.  
⏏️(yathā) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ta) 🚹⨀④🔽(ta)🆎(na+pamatta) 🚹⨀④(ātāpī) 🚹⨀④🆎(pahita+atta) 🚹⨀④🔽(anta)(viharanta)

1259.7 Evarūpāpi kho me, aggivessana, uppannā sukhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati.

Even such pleasant feeling that arose in me, Aggivessana, did not invade my mind and remain.

1260.1 So evaṃ samāhite citte parisuddhe pariyodāte anaṅgaṇe vigatūpakkilese mudubhūte kammaniye ṭhite āneñjappatte sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhininnāmesiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(samāhita) 🚻⨀⑦(citta) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(parisuddha) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(pariyodāta) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(na+aṅgaṇa) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(vigata+upakkilesa) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(mudu+bhūta) 🚻⨀⑦(kammaniya) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(ṭhita) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(āneñja+patta) 🚹⨂⑥(satta) 🚻⨀④🆎(cuti+upapāta+ñāṇa) 🚻⨀②(citta) 🟠⨀👆⏮(abhininnāmeti)  
I | thus | focused | when my mind was | purified | bright | unblemished | rid of imperfection | malleable | wieldy | steady | attained to imperturbability | of beings | to knowledge of the passing away and reappearance | mind | I directed  
When my mind was thus focused, purified, bright, unblemished, rid of imperfection, malleable, wieldy, steady, and attained to imperturbability, I directed it to knowledge of the passing away and reappearance of beings.

1260.2 So dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena satte passāmi cavamāne upapajjamāne hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate yathākammūpage satte pajānāmi…pe…  ayaṃ kho me, aggivessana, rattiyā majjhime yāme dutiyā vijjā adhigatā;  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) 🚻⨀③(dibba) 🚻⨀③(cakkhu) 🚻⨀③🔽(ta)(visuddha) 🚻⨀③🆎(atikkanta+mānusaka) 🚹⨂②(satta) 🟢⨀👆▶️(passati) 🚹⨂②🔽(māna)(cavamāna) 🚹⨂②🔽(māna)(upapajjamāna) 🚹⨂②(hīna) 🚹⨂②(paṇīta) 🚹⨂②🆎(su+vaṇṇa) 🚹⨂②🆎(dur+vaṇṇa) 🚹⨂②🆎(su+gata) 🚹⨂②🆎(dur+gata) 🚹⨂②🆎(yathā+kamma+upaga) 🚹⨂②(satta) 🟢⨀👆▶️(pajānāti) ⏏️(peyyāla) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(ima) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨀⑥(ratti) 🚹⨀⑦(majjhima) 🚹⨀⑦(yāma) 🚺⨀①(dutiya) 🚺⨀①(vijjā) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(adhigata)  
I | with the divine | eye | purified | surpassing the human | beings | I see | passing away | reappearing | inferior | superior | fair | ugly | fortunate | unfortunate | passing on according to their actions | beings | I know clearly | (and so on) | this | indeed | by me | Aggivessana | of the night | in the middle | watch | second | knowledge | attained  
With the divine eye, which is purified and surpasses the human, I saw beings passing away and reappearing, inferior and superior, fair and ugly, fortunate and unfortunate. I understood how beings pass on according to their actions… This was the second knowledge attained by me, Aggivessana, in the middle watch of the night.

1261.1 So evaṃ samāhite citte parisuddhe pariyodāte anaṅgaṇe vigatūpakkilese mudubhūte kammaniye ṭhite āneñjappatte āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhininnāmesiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(samāhita) 🚻⨀⑦(citta) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(parisuddha) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(pariyodāta) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(na+aṅgaṇa) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(vigata+upakkilesa) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(mudu+bhūta) 🚻⨀⑦(kammaniya) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(ṭhita) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(āneñja+patta) 🚹⨂⑥(āsava) 🚻⨀④🆎(khaya+ñāṇa) 🚻⨀②(citta) 🟠⨀👆⏮(abhininnāmeti)

When my mind was thus focused, purified, bright, unblemished, rid of imperfection, malleable, wieldy, steady, and attained to imperturbability, I directed it to knowledge of the destruction of the `āsavā` (defilements).

1261.2 So ‘idaṃ dukkhan’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayo’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhanirodho’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ima) 🚻⨀①(dukkha) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️🆎(yathā+bhūta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(abhijānāti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①🆎(dukkha+samudaya) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️🆎(yathā+bhūta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(abhijānāti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①🆎(dukkha+nirodha) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️🆎(yathā+bhūta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(abhijānāti) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(ima) 🚺⨀①🆎(dukkha+nirodha+gāmī) 🚺⨀①(paṭipadā) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️🆎(yathā+bhūta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(abhijānāti)  
I | this | suffering | (end quote) | as it really is | I experientially understood | this | the origin of suffering | (end quote) | as it really is | I experientially understood | this | the cessation of suffering | (end quote) | as it really is | I experientially understood | this | leading to the cessation of suffering | path | (end quote) | as it really is | I experientially understood  
I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is `dukkha` (suffering)’; I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is the origin of suffering’; I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is the cessation of suffering’; I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is the path leading to the cessation of suffering.’

1261.3 ‘Ime āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayo’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavanirodho’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavanirodhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ima) 🚹⨂①(āsava) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️🆎(yathā+bhūta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(abhijānāti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①🆎(āsava+samudaya) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️🆎(yathā+bhūta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(abhijānāti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①🆎(āsava+nirodha) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️🆎(yathā+bhūta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(abhijānāti) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(ima) 🚺⨀①🆎(āsava+nirodha+gāmī) 🚺⨀①(paṭipadā) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️🆎(yathā+bhūta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(abhijānāti)  
these | the defilements | (end quote) | as it really is | I experientially understood | this | the origin of the defilements | (end quote) | as it really is | I experientially understood | this | the cessation of the defilements | (end quote) | as it really is | I experientially understood | this | leading to the cessation of the defilements | path | (end quote) | as it really is | I experientially understood  
I experientially understood as it really is: ‘These are the defilements’; I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is the origin of the defilements’; I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is the cessation of the defilements’; I experientially understood as it really is: ‘This is the path leading to the cessation of the defilements.’

1261.4 Tassa me evaṃ jānato evaṃ passato kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuccittha, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuccittha, avijjāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuccittha.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(anta)(jānanta) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(anta)(passanta) 🚹⨀⑤🆎(kāma+āsava) ⏏️(api) 🚻⨀①(citta) 🔵⨀🤟⏮(vimuccati) 🚹⨀⑤🆎(bhava+āsava) ⏏️(api) 🚻⨀①(citta) 🔵⨀🤟⏮(vimuccati) 🚹⨀⑤🆎(avijjā+āsava) ⏏️(api) 🚻⨀①(citta) 🔵⨀🤟⏮(vimuccati)  
of that | my | thus | knowing | thus | seeing | from the defilement of sensual pleasure | too | mind | was liberated | from the defilement of existence | too | mind | was liberated | from the defilement of ignorance | too | mind | was liberated  
As I knew and saw this, my mind was liberated from the defilement of sensual pleasure, from the defilement of existence, and from the defilement of ignorance.

1261.5 Vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ ahosi.  
🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(vimutta) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(vimutta) ⏏️(iti) 🚻⨀①(ñāṇa) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti)  
in liberation | liberated | (end quote) | knowledge | there was  
In liberation there was the knowledge: ‘liberated’.

1261.6 ‘Khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhaññāsiṃ.  
🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(khīṇa) 🚺⨀①(jāti) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(vusita) 🚻⨀①🆎(brahma+cariya) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(kata) 🚻⨀①🔽(anīya)(karaṇīya) ⏏️(na) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(apara) 🚻⨀④(itthatta) ⏏️(iti) 🟢⨀👆⏮(abhijānāti)

I experientially understood: ‘Birth (of suffering) is finished, the pure life is lived, what had to be done is done, there is nothing further for this present state of existence.’

1261.7 Ayaṃ kho me, aggivessana, rattiyā pacchime yāme tatiyā vijjā adhigatā;

## Conclusion

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
1262\. Moreover, I recall, Aggivessana, having taught the Dhamma to an assembly of many hundreds. And each and every one thinks of me thus:

‘The wanderer Gotama teaches the Dhamma just for me.’

But, Aggivessana, it should not be seen that way; a Tathāgata teaches the Dhamma to others only for the purpose of making them understand. So, Aggivessana, at the conclusion of that very talk, I settle, steady, unify, and focus my mind internally on that same previous `samādhinimitta` (object of focus), by which I abide continually.”

> 1263\. “This is believable of Bho Gotama, as it is of a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one. But does Bho Gotama recall having slept during the day?”

> “I recall, Aggivessana, in the last month of the hot season, after my meal, having returned from my alms round, I would lay out my four-folded outer robe and lie down on my right side, aware and clearly comprehending, and fall asleep.”

> “Some religious practitioners, Bho Gotama, call this ‘abiding in delusion’.”

> “Aggivessana, one is not deluded or undeluded merely by that. However, Aggivessana, listen to how one is deluded and how one is undeluded. Attend closely, I shall speak.”

“Yes, bho,” Saccaka the Nigaṇṭha’s son replied to the Bhagavā. The Bhagavā said this:

> 1264\. “Aggivessana, whoever has defilements that lead to corruption, to renewed existence, troublesome, resulting in suffering, and lead to future birth, ageing, and death, which are not given up — that person I call ‘deluded’. For, Aggivessana, by not giving up the defilements, one is deluded. But whoever, Aggivessana, has defilements that lead to corruption, to renewed existence, troublesome, resulting in suffering, and lead to future birth, ageing, and death, which are given up — that person I call ‘undeluded’. For, Aggivessana, by giving up the defilements, one is undeluded.
>
> 1265\. For the Tathāgata, Aggivessana, the defilements that lead to corruption, to renewed existence, troublesome, resulting in suffering, and lead to future birth, ageing, and death, are given up, cut off at the root, made like a palm stump, obliterated, and not subject to future arising. Just as, Aggivessana, a palm tree whose crown has been cut off is incapable of growing again, so too for the Tathāgata, Aggivessana, the defilements that lead to corruption, to renewed existence, troublesome, resulting in suffering, and lead to future birth, ageing, and death, are given up, cut off at the root, made like a palm stump, obliterated, and not subject to future arising.”

1266\. When this was said, Saccaka the Nigaṇṭha’s son said this to the Bhagavā:

> “It is incredible, Bho Gotama! It is amazing, Bho Gotama! To think that when Bho Gotama is spoken to with such repeated harshness, being addressed with such aggressive words, his skin colour brightens, and his facial complexion becomes bright, as it should for a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one. I recall, Bho Gotama, having engaged Pūraṇa Kassapa in debate. But when I engaged him in debate, he evaded the issue, diverted the conversation, and displayed anger, ill-will, and indignation. But when Bho Gotama is spoken to with such repeated harshness, being addressed with such aggressive words, his skin colour brightens, and his facial complexion becomes bright, as it should for a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one. I recall, Bho Gotama, having engaged Makkhali Gosāla... Ajita Kesakambala... Pakudha Kaccāyana... Sañjaya Belaṭṭhaputta... and Nigaṇṭha Nāṭaputta in debate. But when I engaged him in debate, he evaded the issue, diverted the conversation, and displayed anger, ill-will, and indignation. But when Bho Gotama is spoken to with such repeated harshness, being addressed with such aggressive words, his skin colour brightens, and his facial complexion becomes bright, as it should for a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one. Well then, Bho Gotama, I must go. I have many duties, much to do.”

> “Aggivessana, do now as you see fit.”

1267\. Then Saccaka the Nigaṇṭha’s son, having rejoiced in and approved of the Bhagavā’s words, rose from his seat and departed.

---

1268\. The Greater Discourse to Saccaka, the sixth, is finished.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
1262\. Abhijānāmi kho panāhaṃ, aggivessana, anekasatāya parisāya dhammaṃ desetā. Apissu maṃ ekameko evaṃ maññati—  ‘mamevārabbha samaṇo gotamo dhammaṃ desetī’ti. Na kho panetaṃ, aggivessana, evaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ; yāvadeva viññāpanatthāya tathāgato paresaṃ dhammaṃ deseti. So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, tassāyeva kathāya pariyosāne, tasmiṃyeva purimasmiṃ samādhinimitte ajjhattameva cittaṃ saṇṭhapemi sannisādemi ekodiṃ karomi samādahāmi, yena sudaṃ niccakappaṃ viharāmī”ti.

1263\. “Okappaniyametaṃ bhoto gotamassa yathā taṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa. Abhijānāti kho pana bhavaṃ gotamo divā supitā”ti? “Abhijānāmahaṃ, aggivessana, gimhānaṃ pacchime māse pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ paññapetvā dakkhiṇena passena sato sampajāno niddaṃ okkamitā”ti. “Etaṃ kho, bho gotama, eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammohavihārasmiṃ vadantī”ti? “Na kho, aggivessana, ettāvatā sammūḷho vā hoti asammūḷho vā. Api ca, aggivessana, yathā sammūḷho ca hoti asammūḷho ca, taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karohi, bhāsissāmī”ti. “Evaṃ, bho”ti kho saccako nigaṇṭhaputto bhagavato paccassosi. Bhagavā etadavoca—

1264\. “Yassa kassaci, aggivessana, ye āsavā saṃkilesikā ponobbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā appahīnā, tamahaṃ ‘sammūḷho’ti vadāmi. Āsavānañhi, aggivessana, appahānā sammūḷho hoti. Yassa kassaci, aggivessana, ye āsavā saṃkilesikā ponobbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā pahīnā, tamahaṃ ‘asammūḷho’ti vadāmi. Āsavānañhi, aggivessana, pahānā asammūḷho hoti.

1265\. Tathāgatassa kho, aggivessana, ye āsavā saṃkilesikā ponobbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā. Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, tālo matthakacchinno abhabbo puna virūḷhiyā; evameva kho, aggivessana, tathāgatassa ye āsavā saṃkilesikā ponobbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā”ti.

1266\. Evaṃ vutte, saccako nigaṇṭhaputto bhagavantaṃ etadavoca—  “acchariyaṃ, bho gotama, abbhutaṃ, bho gotama. Yāvañcidaṃ bhoto gotamassa evaṃ āsajja āsajja vuccamānassa, upanītehi vacanappathehi samudācariyamānassa, chavivaṇṇo ceva pariyodāyati, mukhavaṇṇo ca vippasīdati, yathā taṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa. Abhijānāmahaṃ, bho gotama, pūraṇaṃ kassapaṃ vādena vādaṃ samārabhitā. Sopi mayā vādena vādaṃ samāraddho aññenaññaṃ paṭicari, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmesi, kopañca dosañca appaccayañca pātvākāsi. Bhoto pana gotamassa evaṃ āsajja āsajja vuccamānassa, upanītehi vacanappathehi samudācariyamānassa, chavivaṇṇo ceva pariyodāyati, mukhavaṇṇo ca vippasīdati, yathā taṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa. Abhijānāmahaṃ, bho gotama, makkhaliṃ gosālaṃ…pe…  ajitaṃ kesakambalaṃ…  pakudhaṃ kaccāyanaṃ…  sañjayaṃ belaṭṭhaputtaṃ…  nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ vādena vādaṃ samārabhitā. Sopi mayā vādena vādaṃ samāraddho aññenaññaṃ paṭicari, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmesi, kopañca dosañca appaccayañca pātvākāsi. Bhoto pana gotamassa evaṃ āsajja āsajja vuccamānassa, upanītehi vacanappathehi samudācariyamānassa, chavivaṇṇo ceva pariyodāyati, mukhavaṇṇo ca vippasīdati, yathā taṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa. Handa ca dāni mayaṃ, bho gotama, gacchāma. Bahukiccā mayaṃ, bahukaraṇīyā”ti. “Yassadāni tvaṃ, aggivessana, kālaṃ maññasī”ti.

1267\. Atha kho saccako nigaṇṭhaputto bhagavato bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumoditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmīti.

---

1268\. Mahāsaccakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
1262\. 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀅𑀦𑁂𑀓𑀲𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀸𑀬 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀢𑀸. 𑀅𑀧𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀫𑀁 𑀏𑀓𑀫𑁂𑀓𑁄 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀫𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀢𑀺—  ‘𑀫𑀫𑁂𑀯𑀸𑀭𑀩𑁆𑀪 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺. 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦𑁂𑀢𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀤𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀁; 𑀬𑀸𑀯𑀤𑁂𑀯 𑀯𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀧𑀦𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀣𑀸𑀕𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀭𑁂𑀲𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀓𑀣𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀲𑀸𑀦𑁂, 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀲𑀡𑁆𑀞𑀧𑁂𑀫𑀺 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀺𑀲𑀸𑀤𑁂𑀫𑀺 𑀏𑀓𑁄𑀤𑀺𑀁 𑀓𑀭𑁄𑀫𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀤𑀳𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀼𑀤𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀓𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻”𑀢𑀺.

1263\. “𑀑𑀓𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀦𑀺𑀬𑀫𑁂𑀢𑀁 𑀪𑁄𑀢𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀬𑀣𑀸 𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀭𑀳𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀲𑁆𑀲. 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀢𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀪𑀯𑀁 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄 𑀤𑀺𑀯𑀸 𑀲𑀼𑀧𑀺𑀢𑀸”𑀢𑀺? “𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀳𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀕𑀺𑀫𑁆𑀳𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀫𑁂 𑀫𑀸𑀲𑁂 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸𑀪𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀧𑀺𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀧𑀸𑀢𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀘𑀢𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀼𑀡𑀁 𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀖𑀸𑀝𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀧𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀤𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀡𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀦𑀺𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀁 𑀑𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑀸”𑀢𑀺. “𑀏𑀢𑀁 𑀔𑁄, 𑀪𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫, 𑀏𑀓𑁂 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄𑀳𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀯𑀤𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀻”𑀢𑀺? “𑀦 𑀔𑁄, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀽𑀍𑀳𑁄 𑀯𑀸 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀽𑀍𑀳𑁄 𑀯𑀸. 𑀅𑀧𑀺 𑀘, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀬𑀣𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀽𑀍𑀳𑁄 𑀘 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺 𑀅𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀽𑀍𑀳𑁄 𑀘, 𑀢𑀁 𑀲𑀼𑀡𑀸𑀳𑀺, 𑀲𑀸𑀥𑀼𑀓𑀁 𑀫𑀦𑀲𑀺 𑀓𑀭𑁄𑀳𑀺, 𑀪𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀻”𑀢𑀺. “𑀏𑀯𑀁, 𑀪𑁄”𑀢𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀓𑁄 𑀦𑀺𑀕𑀡𑁆𑀞𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁄𑀲𑀺. 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘—

1264\. “𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀘𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀬𑁂 𑀆𑀲𑀯𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀧𑁄𑀦𑁄𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀯𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀲𑀤𑀭𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑀺𑀧𑀸𑀓𑀸 𑀆𑀬𑀢𑀺𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀳𑀻𑀦𑀸, 𑀢𑀫𑀳𑀁 ‘𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀽𑀍𑀳𑁄’𑀢𑀺 𑀯𑀤𑀸𑀫𑀺. 𑀆𑀲𑀯𑀸𑀦𑀜𑁆𑀳𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀳𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀽𑀍𑀳𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺. 𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀘𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀬𑁂 𑀆𑀲𑀯𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀧𑁄𑀦𑁄𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀯𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀲𑀤𑀭𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑀺𑀧𑀸𑀓𑀸 𑀆𑀬𑀢𑀺𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀳𑀻𑀦𑀸, 𑀢𑀫𑀳𑀁 ‘𑀅𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀽𑀍𑀳𑁄’𑀢𑀺 𑀯𑀤𑀸𑀫𑀺. 𑀆𑀲𑀯𑀸𑀦𑀜𑁆𑀳𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀧𑀳𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀅𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀽𑀍𑀳𑁄 𑀳𑁄𑀢𑀺.

1265\. 𑀢𑀣𑀸𑀕𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀔𑁄, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀬𑁂 𑀆𑀲𑀯𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀧𑁄𑀦𑁄𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀯𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀲𑀤𑀭𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑀺𑀧𑀸𑀓𑀸 𑀆𑀬𑀢𑀺𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀳𑀻𑀦𑀸 𑀉𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀫𑀽𑀮𑀸 𑀢𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀼𑀓𑀢𑀸 𑀅𑀦𑀪𑀸𑀯𑀁𑀓𑀢𑀸 𑀆𑀬𑀢𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀤𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀢𑀸𑀮𑁄 𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀪𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁄 𑀧𑀼𑀦 𑀯𑀺𑀭𑀽𑀍𑀳𑀺𑀬𑀸; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀢𑀣𑀸𑀕𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀬𑁂 𑀆𑀲𑀯𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀧𑁄𑀦𑁄𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀯𑀺𑀓𑀸 𑀲𑀤𑀭𑀸 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑀺𑀧𑀸𑀓𑀸 𑀆𑀬𑀢𑀺𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀳𑀻𑀦𑀸 𑀉𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀫𑀽𑀮𑀸 𑀢𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀼𑀓𑀢𑀸 𑀅𑀦𑀪𑀸𑀯𑀁𑀓𑀢𑀸 𑀆𑀬𑀢𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀤𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸”𑀢𑀺.

1266\. 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀓𑁄 𑀦𑀺𑀕𑀡𑁆𑀞𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘—  “𑀅𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁, 𑀪𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫, 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀼𑀢𑀁, 𑀪𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫. 𑀬𑀸𑀯𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀺𑀤𑀁 𑀪𑁄𑀢𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀆𑀲𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀆𑀲𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲, 𑀉𑀧𑀦𑀻𑀢𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀯𑀘𑀦𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀣𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀸𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲, 𑀙𑀯𑀺𑀯𑀡𑁆𑀡𑁄 𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀤𑀸𑀬𑀢𑀺, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀯𑀡𑁆𑀡𑁄 𑀘 𑀯𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑀻𑀤𑀢𑀺, 𑀬𑀣𑀸 𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀭𑀳𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀲𑁆𑀲. 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫, 𑀧𑀽𑀭𑀡𑀁 𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀧𑀁 𑀯𑀸𑀤𑁂𑀦 𑀯𑀸𑀤𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀭𑀪𑀺𑀢𑀸. 𑀲𑁄𑀧𑀺 𑀫𑀬𑀸 𑀯𑀸𑀤𑁂𑀦 𑀯𑀸𑀤𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑁂𑀦𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀁 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀘𑀭𑀺, 𑀩𑀳𑀺𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸 𑀓𑀣𑀁 𑀅𑀧𑀦𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀲𑀺, 𑀓𑁄𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀤𑁄𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀬𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀧𑀸𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀓𑀸𑀲𑀺. 𑀪𑁄𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀆𑀲𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀆𑀲𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲, 𑀉𑀧𑀦𑀻𑀢𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀯𑀘𑀦𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀣𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀸𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲, 𑀙𑀯𑀺𑀯𑀡𑁆𑀡𑁄 𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀤𑀸𑀬𑀢𑀺, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀯𑀡𑁆𑀡𑁄 𑀘 𑀯𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑀻𑀤𑀢𑀺, 𑀬𑀣𑀸 𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀭𑀳𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀲𑁆𑀲. 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫, 𑀫𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀮𑀺𑀁 𑀕𑁄𑀲𑀸𑀮𑀁…𑀧𑁂…  𑀅𑀚𑀺𑀢𑀁 𑀓𑁂𑀲𑀓𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀮𑀁…  𑀧𑀓𑀼𑀥𑀁 𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀸𑀬𑀦𑀁…  𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀚𑀬𑀁 𑀩𑁂𑀮𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁…  𑀦𑀺𑀕𑀡𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀦𑀸𑀝𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀯𑀸𑀤𑁂𑀦 𑀯𑀸𑀤𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀭𑀪𑀺𑀢𑀸. 𑀲𑁄𑀧𑀺 𑀫𑀬𑀸 𑀯𑀸𑀤𑁂𑀦 𑀯𑀸𑀤𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀭𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑁂𑀦𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀁 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀘𑀭𑀺, 𑀩𑀳𑀺𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸 𑀓𑀣𑀁 𑀅𑀧𑀦𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀲𑀺, 𑀓𑁄𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀤𑁄𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀬𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀧𑀸𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀓𑀸𑀲𑀺. 𑀪𑁄𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀆𑀲𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀆𑀲𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲, 𑀉𑀧𑀦𑀻𑀢𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀯𑀘𑀦𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀣𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀸𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲, 𑀙𑀯𑀺𑀯𑀡𑁆𑀡𑁄 𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀤𑀸𑀬𑀢𑀺, 𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀯𑀡𑁆𑀡𑁄 𑀘 𑀯𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑀻𑀤𑀢𑀺, 𑀬𑀣𑀸 𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀭𑀳𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀲𑁆𑀲. 𑀳𑀦𑁆𑀤 𑀘 𑀤𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀫𑀬𑀁, 𑀪𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫, 𑀕𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸𑀫. 𑀩𑀳𑀼𑀓𑀺𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀸 𑀫𑀬𑀁, 𑀩𑀳𑀼𑀓𑀭𑀡𑀻𑀬𑀸”𑀢𑀺. “𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀤𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁, 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀯𑁂𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦, 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀁 𑀫𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀲𑀻”𑀢𑀺.

1267\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀓𑁄 𑀦𑀺𑀕𑀡𑁆𑀞𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀢𑁄 𑀪𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀫𑁄𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸𑀬𑀸𑀲𑀦𑀸 𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺.

---

1268\. 𑀫𑀳𑀸𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀓𑀲𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀢𑀁 𑀙𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁.
</TabItem>
<TabItem label="Interlinear">
1262.1 Abhijānāmi kho panāhaṃ, aggivessana, anekasatāya parisāya dhammaṃ desetā.  
🟢⨀👆▶️(abhijānāti) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(pana) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚺⨀④🆎(aneka+sata) 🚺⨀④(parisā) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀①(desetar)  
I recall | indeed | moreover | I | Aggivessana | of many hundreds | to an assembly | the Dhamma | a teacher  
Moreover, I recall, Aggivessana, having taught the Dhamma to an assembly of many hundreds.

1262.2 Apissu maṃ ekameko evaṃ maññati—  ‘mamevārabbha samaṇo gotamo dhammaṃ desetī’ti.  
⏏️(api) ⏏️(ssu) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①🆎(eka+eka) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(maññati) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(ārabbha) 🚹⨀①(samaṇa) 🚹⨀①(gotama) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟠⨀🤟▶️(deseti) ⏏️(iti)  
and | furthermore | of me | each and every one | thus | thinks | me | just | concerning | the wanderer | Gotama | the Dhamma | teaches | (end quote)  
And each and every one thinks of me thus: ‘The wanderer Gotama teaches the Dhamma just for me.’

1262.3 Na kho panetaṃ, aggivessana, evaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ;  
⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(pana) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀①🔽(tabba)(daṭṭhabba)  
not | indeed | but | this | Aggivessana | that way | should be seen  
But, Aggivessana, it should not be seen that way;

1262.4 yāvadeva viññāpanatthāya tathāgato paresaṃ dhammaṃ deseti.  
⏏️(yāva) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀④🆎(viññāpana+attha) 🚹⨀①🆎(tathā+gata) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂④(para) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟠⨀🤟▶️(deseti)  
as far as | only | for the purpose of making understand | a Tathāgata | to others | the Dhamma | teaches  
a Tathāgata teaches the Dhamma to others only for the purpose of making them understand.

1262.5 So kho ahaṃ, aggivessana, tassāyeva kathāya pariyosāne, tasmiṃyeva purimasmiṃ samādhinimitte ajjhattameva cittaṃ saṇṭhapemi sannisādemi ekodiṃ karomi samādahāmi, yena sudaṃ niccakappaṃ viharāmī”ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀⑥(ta) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀⑥(kathā) 🚻⨀⑦(pariyosāna) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀⑦(ta) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀⑦(purima) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(samādhi+nimitta) ⏏️(ajjhattaṃ) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀②(citta) 🟠⨀👆▶️(saṇṭhapeti) 🟠⨀👆▶️(sannisādeti) 🚻⨀②(ekodi) 🟢⨀👆▶️(karoti) 🟢⨀👆▶️(samādahati) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀③(ya) ⏏️(sudaṃ) ⏏️(niccakappaṃ) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti)  
that | indeed | I | Aggivessana | of that | very | talk | at the conclusion | in that | same | previous | object of focus | internally | just | mind | I steady | I settle | unified | I make | I focus | by which | indeed | continually | I abide | (end quote)  
So, Aggivessana, at the conclusion of that very talk, I settle, steady, unify, and focus my mind internally on that same previous `samādhinimitta` (object of focus), by which I abide continually.”

1263.1 “Okappaniyametaṃ bhoto gotamassa yathā taṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa.  
🚻⨀①🔽(anīya)(okappaniya) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🚹⨀⑥(bhavant) 🚹⨀⑥(gotama) ⏏️(yathā) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ta) 🚹⨀⑥(arahant) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(sammā+sambuddha)  
believable | this | of Bho | of Gotama | as | that | of a liberated one | of a perfectly awakened one  
“This is believable of Bho Gotama, as it is of a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one.

1263.2 Abhijānāti kho pana bhavaṃ gotamo divā supitā”ti?  
🟢⨀🤟▶️(abhijānāti) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(pana) 🚹⨀①(bhavant) 🚹⨀①(gotama) ⏏️(divā) 🚹⨀①(supitar) ⏏️(iti)  
recalls | indeed | but | Bho | Gotama | by day | a sleeper | (end quote)  
But does Bho Gotama recall having slept during the day?”

1263.3 “Abhijānāmahaṃ, aggivessana, gimhānaṃ pacchime māse pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ paññapetvā dakkhiṇena passena sato sampajāno niddaṃ okkamitā”ti.  
🟢⨀👆▶️(abhijānāti) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨂⑥(gimhā) 🚹⨀⑦(pacchima) 🚹⨀⑦(māsa) ⏏️(pacchābhattaṃ) 🚹⨀①🆎(piṇḍa+pāta+paṭikkanta) 🚺⨀②🆎(catu+guṇa) 🚺⨀②(saṅghāṭi) 🔽(tvā)(paññāpeti) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀③(dakkhiṇa) 🚻⨀③(passa) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(sata) 🚹⨀①(sampajāna) 🚺⨀②(niddā) 🚹⨀①(okkamitar) ⏏️(iti)  
I recall | I | Aggivessana | of the hot season | in the last | month | after the meal | returned from the alms round | four-folded | outer robe | having laid out | on the right | side | aware | clearly comprehending | sleep | one who fell into | (end quote)  
“I recall, Aggivessana, in the last month of the hot season, after my meal, having returned from my alms round, I would lay out my four-folded outer robe and lie down on my right side, aware and clearly comprehending, and fall asleep.”

1263.4 “Etaṃ kho, bho gotama, eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammohavihārasmiṃ vadantī”ti?  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⓪(gotama) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(eka) 🚹⨂①🆎(samaṇa+brāhmaṇa) 🚹⨀⑦🆎(sammoha+vihāra) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(vadati) ⏏️(iti)  
this | indeed | Bho | Gotama | some | religious practitioners | as abiding in delusion | call | (end quote)  
“Some religious practitioners, Bho Gotama, call this ‘abiding in delusion’.”

1263.5 “Na kho, aggivessana, ettāvatā sammūḷho vā hoti asammūḷho vā.  
⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) ⏏️(ettāvatā) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(sammūḷha) ⏏️(vā) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)🆎(na+sammūḷha) ⏏️(vā)  
not | indeed | Aggivessana | to this extent | deluded | or | one is | undeluded | or  
“Aggivessana, one is not deluded or undeluded merely by that.

1263.6 Api ca, aggivessana, yathā sammūḷho ca hoti asammūḷho ca, taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karohi, bhāsissāmī”ti.  
⏏️(api) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) ⏏️(yathā) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(sammūḷha) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)🆎(na+sammūḷha) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(ta) 🟢⨀🤘⏹(suṇāti) ⏏️(sādhukaṃ) 🚹⨀⑦(manas) 🟢⨀🤘⏹(karoti) 🟢⨀👆⏭(bhāsati) ⏏️(iti)  
but | rather | Aggivessana | how | deluded | and | one is | undeluded | and | that | listen to | closely | in mind | make | I shall speak | (end quote)  
However, Aggivessana, listen to how one is deluded and how one is undeluded. Attend closely, I shall speak.”

1263.7 “Evaṃ, bho”ti kho saccako nigaṇṭhaputto bhagavato paccassosi.  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(saccaka) 🚹⨀①🆎(nigaṇṭha+putta) 🚹⨀④(bhagavant) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(paṭissuṇāti)  
yes | Bho | (end quote) | indeed | Saccaka | the Nigaṇṭha’s son | to the Bhagavā | replied  
“Yes, Bho,” Saccaka the Nigaṇṭha’s son replied to the Bhagavā.

1264.1 “Yassa kassaci, aggivessana, ye āsavā saṃkilesikā ponobbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā appahīnā, tamahaṃ ‘sammūḷho’ti vadāmi.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(ya) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(kaci) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ya) 🚹⨂①(āsava) 🚹⨂①(saṃkilesika) 🚹⨂①(ponobbhavika) 🚹⨂①(sadara) 🚹⨂①🆎(dukkha+vipāka) ⏏️(āyatiṃ) 🚹⨂①🔽(iya)🆎(jāti+jarā+maraṇa) 🚹⨂①🔽(ta)🆎(na+pahīna) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(sammūḷha) ⏏️(iti) 🟢⨀👆▶️(vadati)

“Aggivessana, whoever has defilements that lead to corruption, to renewed existence, troublesome, resulting in suffering, and lead to future birth, ageing, and death, which are not given up — that person I call ‘deluded’.

1264.2 Āsavānañhi, aggivessana, appahānā sammūḷho hoti.

of the defilements | for | Aggivessana | by not giving up | deluded | one is
