1264.3 Yassa kassaci, aggivessana, ye āsavā saṃkilesikā ponobbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā pahīnā, tamahaṃ ‘asammūḷho’ti vadāmi.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(ya) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(kaci) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ya) 🚹⨂①(āsava) 🚹⨂①(saṃkilesika) 🚹⨂①(ponobbhavika) 🚹⨂①(sadara) 🚹⨂①🆎(dukkha+vipāka) ⏏️(āyatiṃ) 🚹⨂①🔽(iya)🆎(jāti+jarā+maraṇa) 🚹⨂①🔽(ta)(pahīna) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)🆎(na+sammūḷha) ⏏️(iti) 🟢⨀👆▶️(vadati)  
of whoever | of anyone | Aggivessana | which | defilements | leading to corruption | leading to renewed existence | troublesome | resulting in suffering | in future | leading to birth, ageing and death | given up | that person | I | undeluded | (end quote) | I call  
But whoever, Aggivessana, has defilements that lead to corruption, to renewed existence, troublesome, resulting in suffering, and lead to future birth, ageing, and death, which are given up — that person I call ‘undeluded’.

1264.4 Āsavānañhi, aggivessana, pahānā asammūḷho hoti.  
🚹⨂⑥(āsava) ⏏️(hi) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚻⨀⑤(pahāna) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)🆎(na+sammūḷha) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(hoti)  
of the defilements | for | Aggivessana | by giving up | undeluded | one is  
For, Aggivessana, by giving up the defilements, one is undeluded.

1265.1 Tathāgatassa kho, aggivessana, ye āsavā saṃkilesikā ponobbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā.  
🚹⨀⑥🆎(tathā+gata) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ya) 🚹⨂①(āsava) 🚹⨂①(saṃkilesika) 🚹⨂①(ponobbhavika) 🚹⨂①(sadara) 🚹⨂①🆎(dukkha+vipāka) ⏏️(āyatiṃ) 🚹⨂①🔽(iya)🆎(jāti+jarā+maraṇa) 🚹⨂①🔽(ta)(pahīna) 🚹⨂①🆎(ucchinna+mūla) 🚹⨂①🆎(tāla+avatthu+kata) 🚹⨂①🆎(anabhāva+kata) ⏏️(āyatiṃ) 🚹⨂①🆎(na+uppāda+dhamma)  
of the Tathāgata | indeed | Aggivessana | which | defilements | leading to corruption | leading to renewed existence | troublesome | resulting in suffering | in future | leading to birth, ageing and death | given up | cut off at the root | made like a palm stump | obliterated | in future | not subject to arising  
For the Tathāgata, Aggivessana, the defilements that lead to corruption, to renewed existence, troublesome, resulting in suffering, and lead to future birth, ageing, and death, are given up, cut off at the root, made like a palm stump, obliterated, and not subject to future arising.

1265.2 Seyyathāpi, aggivessana, tālo matthakacchinno abhabbo puna virūḷhiyā;  
⏏️(seyyathāpi) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀①(tāla) 🚹⨀①🆎(matthaka+chinna) 🚹⨀①🔽(ṇya)🆎(na+bhabba) ⏏️(puna) 🚺⨀④(virūḷhi)  
just as | Aggivessana | a palm tree | whose crown has been cut off | incapable | again | of growing  
Just as, Aggivessana, a palm tree whose crown has been cut off is incapable of growing again,

1265.3 evameva kho, aggivessana, tathāgatassa ye āsavā saṃkilesikā ponobbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā”ti.  
⏏️(evaṃ) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(tathā+gata) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ya) 🚹⨂①(āsava) 🚹⨂①(saṃkilesika) 🚹⨂①(ponobbhavika) 🚹⨂①(sadara) 🚹⨂①🆎(dukkha+vipāka) ⏏️(āyatiṃ) 🚹⨂①🔽(iya)🆎(jāti+jarā+maraṇa) 🚹⨂①🔽(ta)(pahīna) 🚹⨂①🆎(ucchinna+mūla) 🚹⨂①🆎(tāla+avatthu+kata) 🚹⨂①🆎(anabhāva+kata) ⏏️(āyatiṃ) 🚹⨂①🆎(na+uppāda+dhamma) ⏏️(iti)  
in the same way | just | indeed | Aggivessana | of the Tathāgata | which | defilements | leading to corruption | leading to renewed existence | troublesome | resulting in suffering | in future | leading to birth, ageing and death | given up | cut off at the root | made like a palm stump | obliterated | in future | not subject to arising | (end quote)  
so too for the Tathāgata, Aggivessana, the defilements that lead to corruption, to renewed existence, troublesome, resulting in suffering, and lead to future birth, ageing, and death, are given up, cut off at the root, made like a palm stump, obliterated, and not subject to future arising.”

1266.1 Evaṃ vutte, saccako nigaṇṭhaputto bhagavantaṃ etadavoca—  “acchariyaṃ, bho gotama, abbhutaṃ, bho gotama.  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(vutta) 🚹⨀①(saccaka) 🚹⨀①🆎(nigaṇṭha+putta) 🚹⨀②(bhagavant) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(vadati) 🚻⨀①(acchariya) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⓪(gotama) 🚻⨀①(abbhuta) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⓪(gotama)  
thus | said | Saccaka | the Nigaṇṭha’s son | to the Bhagavā | this | said | incredible | Bho | Gotama | amazing | Bho | Gotama  
When this was said, Saccaka the Nigaṇṭha’s son said this to the Bhagavā: “It is incredible, Bho Gotama! It is amazing, Bho Gotama!

1266.2 Yāvañcidaṃ bhoto gotamassa evaṃ āsajja āsajja vuccamānassa, upanītehi vacanappathehi samudācariyamānassa, chavivaṇṇo ceva pariyodāyati, mukhavaṇṇo ca vippasīdati, yathā taṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa.  
⏏️(yāva) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀⑥(bhavant) 🚹⨀⑥(gotama) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🔽(ya)(āsīdati) 🔽(ya)(āsīdati) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(māna)(vuccamāna) 🚹⨂③🔽(ta)(upanīta) 🚹⨂③🆎(vacana+patha) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(māna)(samudācariyamāna) 🚹⨀①🆎(chavi+vaṇṇa) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(pariyodāyati) 🚹⨀①🆎(mukha+vaṇṇa) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(vippasīdati) ⏏️(yathā) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ta) 🚹⨀⑥(arahant) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(sammā+sambuddha)  
so much so | and | this | of Bho | of Gotama | thus | insulting | insulting | being spoken to | with accusatory | ways of speech | being addressed | skin colour | and | just | brightens | facial complexion | and | becomes bright | as | that | of a liberated one | of a perfectly awakened one  
To think that when Bho Gotama is spoken to with such repeated harshness, being addressed with such aggressive words, his skin colour brightens, and his facial complexion becomes bright, as it should for a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one.

1266.3 Abhijānāmahaṃ, bho gotama, pūraṇaṃ kassapaṃ vādena vādaṃ samārabhitā.  
🟢⨀👆▶️(abhijānāti) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⓪(gotama) 🚹⨀②(pūraṇa) 🚹⨀②(kassapa) 🚹⨀③(vāda) 🚹⨀②(vāda) 🚹⨀①(samārabhitar)  
I recall | I | Bho | Gotama | Pūraṇa | Kassapa | with argument | in debate | one who engaged  
I recall, Bho Gotama, having engaged Pūraṇa Kassapa in debate.

1266.4 Sopi mayā vādena vādaṃ samāraddho aññenaññaṃ paṭicari, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmesi, kopañca dosañca appaccayañca pātvākāsi.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(api) *️⃣👆⚧⨀③(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀③(vāda) 🚹⨀②(vāda) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(samāraddha) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀③(añña) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(añña) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(paṭicarati) ⏏️(bahiddhā) 🚺⨀②(kathā) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(apanāmeti) 🚹⨀②(kopa) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀②(dosa) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀②🆎(na+paccaya) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(pātukaroti)  
he | too | by me | with argument | in debate | engaged | with another | another | evaded | outside | the conversation | diverted | anger | and | ill-will | and | indignation | and | displayed  
But when I engaged him in debate, he evaded the issue, diverted the conversation, and displayed anger, ill-will, and indignation.

1266.5 Bhoto pana gotamassa evaṃ āsajja āsajja vuccamānassa, upanītehi vacanappathehi samudācariyamānassa, chavivaṇṇo ceva pariyodāyati, mukhavaṇṇo ca vippasīdati, yathā taṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa.  
🚹⨀⑥(bhavant) ⏏️(pana) 🚹⨀⑥(gotama) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🔽(ya)(āsīdati) 🔽(ya)(āsīdati) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(māna)(vuccamāna) 🚹⨂③🔽(ta)(upanīta) 🚹⨂③🆎(vacana+patha) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(māna)(samudācariyamāna) 🚹⨀①🆎(chavi+vaṇṇa) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(pariyodāyati) 🚹⨀①🆎(mukha+vaṇṇa) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(vippasīdati) ⏏️(yathā) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ta) 🚹⨀⑥(arahant) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(sammā+sambuddha)  
of Bho | but | of Gotama | thus | insulting | insulting | being spoken to | with accusatory | ways of speech | being addressed | skin colour | and | just | brightens | facial complexion | and | becomes bright | as | that | of a liberated one | of a perfectly awakened one  
But when Bho Gotama is spoken to with such repeated harshness, being addressed with such aggressive words, his skin colour brightens, and his facial complexion becomes bright, as it should for a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one.

1266.6 Abhijānāmahaṃ, bho gotama, makkhaliṃ gosālaṃ…pe…  ajitaṃ kesakambalaṃ…  pakudhaṃ kaccāyanaṃ…  sañjayaṃ belaṭṭhaputtaṃ…  nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ vādena vādaṃ samārabhitā.  
🟢⨀👆▶️(abhijānāti) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⓪(gotama) 🚹⨀②(makkhali) 🚹⨀②(gosāla) ⏏️(peyyāla) 🚹⨀②(ajita) 🚹⨀②(kesakambala) 🚹⨀②(pakudha) 🚹⨀②(kaccāyana) 🚹⨀②(sañjaya) 🚹⨀②(belaṭṭhaputta) 🚹⨀②(nigaṇṭha) 🚹⨀②(nāṭaputta) 🚹⨀③(vāda) 🚹⨀②(vāda) 🚹⨀①(samārabhitar)  
I recall | I | Bho | Gotama | Makkhali | Gosāla | (and so on) | Ajita | Kesakambala | Pakudha | Kaccāyana | Sañjaya | Belaṭṭhaputta | Nigaṇṭha | Nāṭaputta | with argument | in debate | one who engaged  
I recall, Bho Gotama, having engaged Makkhali Gosāla... Ajita Kesakambala... Pakudha Kaccāyana... Sañjaya Belaṭṭhaputta... and Nigaṇṭha Nāṭaputta in debate.

1266.7 Sopi mayā vādena vādaṃ samāraddho aññenaññaṃ paṭicari, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmesi, kopañca dosañca appaccayañca pātvākāsi.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(api) *️⃣👆⚧⨀③(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀③(vāda) 🚹⨀②(vāda) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(samāraddha) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀③(añña) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(añña) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(paṭicarati) ⏏️(bahiddhā) 🚺⨀②(kathā) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(apanāmeti) 🚹⨀②(kopa) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀②(dosa) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀②🆎(na+paccaya) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(pātukaroti)  
he | too | by me | with argument | in debate | engaged | with another | another | evaded | outside | the conversation | diverted | anger | and | ill-will | and | indignation | and | displayed  
But when I engaged him in debate, he evaded the issue, diverted the conversation, and displayed anger, ill-will, and indignation.

1266.8 Bhoto pana gotamassa evaṃ āsajja āsajja vuccamānassa, upanītehi vacanappathehi samudācariyamānassa, chavivaṇṇo ceva pariyodāyati, mukhavaṇṇo ca vippasīdati, yathā taṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa.  
🚹⨀⑥(bhavant) ⏏️(pana) 🚹⨀⑥(gotama) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🔽(ya)(āsīdati) 🔽(ya)(āsīdati) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(māna)(vuccamāna) 🚹⨂③🔽(ta)(upanīta) 🚹⨂③🆎(vacana+patha) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(māna)(samudācariyamāna) 🚹⨀①🆎(chavi+vaṇṇa) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(pariyodāyati) 🚹⨀①🆎(mukha+vaṇṇa) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(vippasīdati) ⏏️(yathā) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ta) 🚹⨀⑥(arahant) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(sammā+sambuddha)  
of Bho | but | of Gotama | thus | insulting | insulting | being spoken to | with accusatory | ways of speech | being addressed | skin colour | and | just | brightens | facial complexion | and | becomes bright | as | that | of a liberated one | of a perfectly awakened one  
But when Bho Gotama is spoken to with such repeated harshness, being addressed with such aggressive words, his skin colour brightens, and his facial complexion becomes bright, as it should for a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one.

1266.9 Handa ca dāni mayaṃ, bho gotama, gacchāma.  
⏏️(handa) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(dāni) *️⃣👆⚧⨂①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⓪(gotama) 🟢⨂👆▶️(gacchati)  
well then | and | now | we | Bho | Gotama | go  
Well then, Bho Gotama, I must go.

1266.10 Bahukiccā mayaṃ, bahukaraṇīyā”ti.  
🚹⨂①🆎(bahu+kicca) *️⃣👆⚧⨂①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂①🆎(bahu+karaṇīya) ⏏️(iti)  
with many duties | we | with much to do | (end quote)  
I have many duties, much to do.”

1266.11 “Yassadāni tvaṃ, aggivessana, kālaṃ maññasī”ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀⑥(ya) ⏏️(dāni) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(aggivessana) 🚹⨀②(kāla) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(maññati) ⏏️(iti)  
of whatever | now | you | Aggivessana | the time | you think | (end quote)  
“Aggivessana, do now as you see fit.”

1267.1 Atha kho saccako nigaṇṭhaputto bhagavato bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumoditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmīti.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(saccaka) 🚹⨀①🆎(nigaṇṭha+putta) 🚹⨀⑥(bhagavant) 🚻⨀②🔽(ta)(bhāsita) 🔽(tvā)(abhinandati) 🔽(tvā)(anumodati) 🔽(ya)(uṭṭhāti) 🚻⨀⑤(āsana) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(pakkamati) ⏏️(iti)  
then | indeed | Saccaka | the Nigaṇṭha’s son | of the Bhagavā | what was spoken | having rejoiced in | having approved of | having risen | from his seat | departed | (end quote)  
Then Saccaka the Nigaṇṭha’s son, having rejoiced in and approved of the Bhagavā’s words, rose from his seat and departed.

1268.1 Mahāsaccakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ.  
🚻⨀①🆎(mahā+saccaka+sutta) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(niṭṭhita) 🚻⨀①(chaṭṭha)

The Greater Discourse to Saccaka, the sixth, is finished.
